Mounted combat (Capability API 1.496). `combat.mounts` lets a combatant mount a willing creature of its own side beside it that is at least `larger` sizes bigger (5e's 1), for half its speed, and get down beside it the same way. The menu offers `move:mount:<id>` and `move:dismount`. The rider goes wherever the mount walks and walks no way of its own while up. With `controlled.standard` (5e's `["dash", "disengage", "dodge"]`), a mount with no sheet is controlled: its turn comes straight after its rider's, and it only moves and takes the standard actions named. One that already had its turn this round waits until the next. A creature whose block says `mountIndependent: true` acts as it will. `fall` (`save`, `difficulty`, `applies`, `when`) is for a mount that comes to hold a condition in `when`, or is pushed: its rider throws the save or comes off with `applies` on, and on a success gets down on their feet. A rider who holds one comes off too. A mount that drops, or a rider who does, parts them. 5e: `{ "larger": 1, "controlled": { "standard": ["dash", "disengage", "dodge"] }, "fall": { "save": "dex_save", "difficulty": 10, "applies": "prone", "when": ["prone"] } }`.

Dragging whoever you hold (Capability API 1.497). A combat condition's `dragged` drags its holder along when whoever put it on walks: each step the walker takes, the holder moves into the cell the walker left. The walker's speed is halved for it, with every step costing double, unless the holder is at least `unlessSmaller` sizes smaller. 5e's grappled: `"dragged": { "unlessSmaller": 2 }`.

Fighting under water (Capability API 1.498). `combat.underwater` names the board tiles that are under water in `terrain`. Whoever stands on one is under water, unless they are up in the air or a flier with no swim speed. `melee.mode` is put on their melee attacks unless they have a swim speed or the weapon is one `melee.unless` names: `names` are matched as a word of the attack's label, `qualities` against its qualities. `ranged.pastNormal: "never"` keeps a shot by or at somebody under water from reaching past the weapon's normal range, and `ranged.mode` is put on shots within it unless `ranged.unless` names the weapon. Damage of a type in `resist` is halved on whoever is under water. `breath` counts their own turns under water. They hold their breath for `holds` turns (`rounds`, plus `perMod.rounds` for each point of an ability's modifier, never fewer than `atLeast`), then last `then` turns more, and drop to 0 as the next one starts. Whoever holds a condition in `exempt.conditions` or carries a tag in `exempt.tags` breathes, and breath comes back out of the water. 5e: `{ "terrain": ["water"], "melee": { "mode": "disadvantage", "unless": { "names": ["dagger", "javelin", "shortsword", "spear", "trident"] } }, "ranged": { "pastNormal": "never", "mode": "disadvantage", "unless": { "names": ["crossbow", "net", "javelin", "spear", "trident", "dart"] } }, "resist": ["fire"], "breath": { "holds": { "rounds": 10, "perMod": { "ability": "con", "rounds": 10 }, "atLeast": 5 }, "then": { "rounds": 0, "perMod": { "ability": "con", "rounds": 1 }, "atLeast": 1 }, "exempt": { "conditions": ["water_breathing"] } } }`.

Jumping (Capability API 1.499). `combat.jumping` gives every combatant on a board a Jump on their move menu (`move:jump`), to one of its `cells`. `long` is how far a long jump goes and `high` what a high jump clears, each an ability's `score` or `modifier` (`of`) plus `plus`, in the ruleset's own distance. Either goes that far only after a run-up of `runUp` walked this turn just before the jump; without one, `standing: "half"` halves it. A jump is a straight line in one of the eight directions, two cells or more, each cell a cell of movement whatever the ground: it goes over hard going, water, pits, friends and ground no higher than the high jump clears, never through a wall or a foe, and lands where its jumper could stand, no more than a level down. Opportunity attacks are taken on the way as on a walk, and lasting areas touch the jumper only where they land. Landing on hard going, they make the `hardLanding` check against its `difficulty` or take its `applies` condition. A condition's `jumps` makes its holder jump further: `times` over (the largest counts), and on a jump with a run-up `plus` an ability's modifier more, to the long jump only with `long: true`. Nobody jumps who rides, is inside somebody, is up in the air, drags somebody, or holds a condition that decides which way they move; a holder of `cannot-approach-source` jumps no nearer its source. The move event carries `jumped: true`. 5e: `{ "long": { "ability": "str", "of": "score" }, "high": { "ability": "str", "of": "modifier", "plus": 3 }, "runUp": 10, "standing": "half", "hardLanding": { "check": "agility", "difficulty": 10, "applies": "prone" } }`; the Jump spell and Boots of Striding and Springing `{ "jumps": { "times": 3 } }`, Step of the Wind `{ "times": 2 }`, Remarkable Athlete `{ "plus": { "ability": "str", "long": true } }` and Second-Story Work `{ "plus": { "ability": "dex" } }`.

Death saves and healing at its most (Capability API 1.500). `combat.dying.asSave: true` makes the dying roll a save: whatever changes every save of its holder's, narrowed to no save, changes it too, both what it adds (Bless, Aura of Protection) and whether it is rolled twice. A condition's `deathSaves: "advantage"` (or `"disadvantage"`) rolls its holder's dying rolls that way, with or without `asSave`; the dying event then carries `mode`, and with `asSave` its `bonuses` and `total`. A natural 1 or 20 is still read off the die kept. A condition's `healedMax: true` makes every healing its holder gets, by an action, a revival, a regeneration or a kill that heals, the highest its dice can come to. 5e: `"asSave": true` on `dying`, and Beacon of Hope's condition `{ "effects": ["own-saves-advantage"], "saves": ["wis_save"], "deathSaves": "advantage", "healedMax": true }`.

A weapon changed for a time (Capability API 1.501). A condition's `weapon` changes its holder's weapon blows while it holds: those whose attack has one of `names` as a word of its label, or whose held item is one of `items` (`names`, `tags`, `notTags`, as `attackRows` reads them), or every weapon blow when it names neither. `toHit` and `damage` are added to the attack and damage rolls. `ability` (or `abilityFrom`, a sheet field holding an ability id, for a spellcasting ability) is the ability the attack is made with instead of its own, its modifier taking the old one's place to hit and, where the damage added the old one, to damage; a creature off a stat block has no ability to swap. `die` makes the damage one die of that many sides, and `qualities` are carried past a resistance. The menu's forecast and the roll both read it. 5e: Magic Weapon `{ "toHit": 1, "damage": 1, "qualities": ["magical"] }` (2 and 3 by slot as separate conditions), Shillelagh `{ "names": ["club", "quarterstaff"], "abilityFrom": "spellcasting_ability", "die": 8, "qualities": ["magical"] }`, Oil of Sharpness `{ "items": { "tags": ["slashing"] }, "toHit": 3, "damage": 3 }`, Sacred Weapon's "becomes magical" `{ "qualities": ["magical"] }`.

Advantage for whoever put it on (Capability API 1.502). An `attacks-taken` modifier with a `mode` may take `fromSource: true`: only attacks by whoever put the condition on roll that way. An `applies` may end with `endsAfter: "attacked-by-source"`: it comes off after the next attack roll its source makes against its holder, and nobody else's. 5e's True Strike applies a condition `{ "modifiers": [{ "to": "attacks-taken", "mode": "advantage", "fromSource": true }] }` to its target with `{ "duration": { "rounds": 1 }, "endsAfter": "attacked-by-source" }`.

Smites (Capability API 1.503). An `applies` may end with `endsAfter: "own-hit"`: it comes off after its holder's next weapon attack that hits, and a miss leaves it on, so the damage a smite's condition adds (a `damage` modifier with `attacks: "weapon"`) lands on that hit and no other. A condition's `onHit` puts each of its `applies` on whoever its holder hits with an attack roll, from the holder, once the blow is over: only a weapon's blow with `weapon: true`, and only a melee or ranged one with `blow`. A condition that holds concentration passes it on to what it puts on. 5e's Branding Smite: `{ "modifiers": [{ "to": "damage", "attacks": "weapon", "dice": "2d6", "damageType": "radiant" }], "onHit": { "weapon": true, "applies": [{ "condition": "branded", "duration": { "rounds": 10 } }] } }`, applied with `"endsAfter": "own-hit"`, where `branded` is `{ "suppresses": ["invisible"] }`.

Elemental forms, and landing from a fallen mount (Capability API 1.504). A condition's `stopsInFoes: true` lets its holder walk into a hostile creature's space and stop there; a friend's space is still only walked through. A condition's `entering` does something to each creature whose space its holder enters, the first time in each turn: `harm` (`dice`, `flat`, `damageType`) with no save, and its `applies` from the holder. `combat.mounts.fall.landsWith` names a budget: a rider whose mount comes to hold a condition of `fall.when` spends one, while they have one, and lands on their feet beside it instead of throwing the save. Being pushed off is still the save. 5e: Air and Water Form `{ "stopsInFoes": true, "passesCreatures": "any" }`, Fire Form `{ "stopsInFoes": true, "entering": { "harm": { "dice": "1d10", "damageType": "fire" }, "applies": [{ "condition": "on_fire", "duration": "until-dispelled" }] } }`, and `"landsWith": "reaction"`.

Illusory duplicates (Capability API 1.505). A condition's `duplicates` gives its holder `count` duplicates that draw attacks made against them. Before each attack roll at the holder, a `sides` die (a d20 unless said) is rolled; at or over `thresholds[left - 1]`, where `left` is how many duplicates are still there, the attack is rolled against a duplicate instead, whose defense is `defense.flat` plus the holder's `defense.ability` modifier. A duplicate the attack hits is destroyed and takes nothing else from it; the condition ends with the last. An attacker who can't see the holder, or who senses them with one of `pierced` within that sense's range, is never fooled. `thresholds` has one entry for each number left, from one up to `count`. 5e's Mirror Image: `{ "count": 3, "thresholds": [11, 8, 6], "defense": { "flat": 10, "ability": "dex" }, "pierced": ["blindsight", "truesight"] }`, applied for 10 rounds.

Blinking out (Capability API 1.506). A condition's `blinks` rolls a `sides` die (a d20 unless said) as each of its holder's turns ends; at or over `atLeast` they vanish from the board until their next turn starts. While gone nobody reaches them and nothing harms them, though what they keep up (concentration, zones, summons) carries on, and the fight is not lost for their being away. As their next turn starts, before anything else of it, they come back where they vanished, or the nearest free place their size fits. 5e's Blink: `{ "condition": "blink", "blinks": { "atLeast": 11 } }`, applied for 10 rounds.

Stopping time (Capability API 1.507). A condition's `stopsTime` (`dice`, `flat` or both) gives its holder that many turns in a row, rolled as the turn it was put on in ends. Each starts straight after the last, with no step along the order, no new round and no window between turns; nobody else acts, answers with a reaction or takes a readied use, and nobody else's durations count down. It ends there and then once its holder reaches another creature: an attack at them, harm they take from the holder, a save the holder has them throw, or a condition the holder puts on them; otherwise it ends after the last turn. The rest of the turn it ended in goes on as usual. 5e's Time Stop, put on its caster: `{ "condition": "time_stop", "stopsTime": { "dice": "1d4", "flat": 1 } }`.

Harm dealt back on a hit (Capability API 1.508). A condition's `whenHit` may now `harms` its holder's hitter (`dice`, `flat`, `damageType`) with no save, once the blow that hit is over, and `within` keeps the whole answer to a hitter that near, in the ruleset's distance. Its `save` and `applies` are now each optional: with no `save`, what it `applies` goes on unasked; a `save` needs `applies`, and it does something, `applies`, `harms` or both. 5e's Fire Shield: `{ "whenHit": { "blow": "melee", "within": 5, "harms": { "dice": "2d8", "damageType": "fire" } } }`, the chill shield's `cold`; an azer's Heated Body through its block's `keepsOn`, `1d10` fire.

Stepping in front of an attack, and defense against one kind (Capability API 1.509). An entry's `takesAim: true`, taken at an `"aimed"` moment `for` an ally or anyone `within` reach, makes its holder the target of what was aimed in place of the one the moment was about; it calls nothing off and deals nothing of its own. An `"aimed"` moment may now carry `blow` (`"melee"` or `"ranged"`), answering only an attack of that kind, and a `defense` modifier may carry `blow` too, counting only against attacks of that kind. 5e's Arrow-Catching Shield: a worn modifier `{ "to": "defense", "flat": 2, "blow": "ranged" }`, and `{ "kind": "utility", "reaction": { "on": "aimed", "for": "ally", "within": 5, "blow": "ranged" }, "takesAim": true }`.

No critical hits (Capability API 1.510). The `criticals-against-normal` effect, on a condition or an item's `worn`, makes a critical hit on its holder a plain hit, whatever made it one: a natural top face, a widened critical range, or the `attacks-from-adjacent-critical` of a paralyzed holder. 5e's Adamantine Armor: `{ "worn": { "effects": ["criticals-against-normal"] } }`.

Weapons without damage dice (Capability API 1.511). An item's `attack.damage` may leave out `dice`: the blow deals its `bonus` alone, or nothing. Its `onHit` may now say `atLeast: 0`, put on by any hit whatever it dealt, and `checkEnds` (`check`, `difficulty`), a check of `combat.checks` its holder may spend the standard budget on to end it. 5e's blowgun: `{ "damage": { "bonus": 1, "type": "piercing" } }`; a net: `{ "damage": {}, "onHit": [{ "condition": "restrained", "atLeast": 0, "targetIs": { "upToSize": "large" }, "checkEnds": { "check": "might", "difficulty": 10 } }] }`.

Breathing only water (Capability API 1.512). `combat.underwater.breath.waterOnly.tags` names the tags of creatures that breathe only water. They hold their breath out of the water the way everybody else holds it under water (`holds`, then `then`, then 0), and it comes back under water. `exempt` spares them as it spares anybody. 5e, for a shark or anything else with Water Breathing and not Amphibious: `{ "waterOnly": { "tags": ["water-breather"] } }`.

Items that keep conditions on and lengthen jumps (Capability API 1.513). An item's `worn` or `carried` effect may say `keepsOn`, up to 4 of the sheet's conditions that are on its holder in a fight for as long as it applies, never written to the sheet, and `jumps`, as a condition's (`times`, or `plus` an ability's modifier). Taken off or dropped, they go. 5e's Boots of Striding and Springing: `{ "worn": { "jumps": { "times": 3 } } }`; an Ioun Stone of Awareness: `{ "worn": { "keepsOn": ["alert"] } }`, with `alert` a condition that keeps its holder from being surprised.

Stopped by damage (Capability API 1.514). A combat condition with `suppressedWhenHarmed: "until-own-turn-start"` does nothing from the moment its holder takes damage until their next turn starts. It is not ended, and its clock runs on. 5e's Cloak of Displacement, worn with `keepsOn: ["displaced"]`: `{ "condition": "displaced", "effects": ["attacks-against-disadvantage"], "suppressedWhenHarmed": "until-own-turn-start" }`.

Abilities a condition changes (Capability API 1.515). A combat condition's `abilities` changes its holder's abilities while it holds, by ability id, as an item's do: `set` lifts a score to at least a number (a higher one stays), `add` goes on top (to no more than `max`), and `to` makes it exactly that number. Held on the sheet, everything the sheet works out reads the changed score. Put on in a fight, the fighter's attacks, saves, checks and defense are worked out again with it, and again when it ends; their health and speed stay as they are. On a stat block with no sheet, its printed `abilities` are changed the same way, and each save and each contest check that reads an ability's modifier moves by as much as that modifier does, back as printed when it ends; a block that prints no scores is left as it is. 5e's Potion of Hill Giant Strength: `{ "condition": "hill_giant_strength", "effects": [], "abilities": { "str": { "set": 21 } } }`; Feeblemind: `"abilities": { "int": { "to": 1 }, "cha": { "to": 1 } }`.

Abilities raised for good (Capability API 1.516). An item's `use` may say `raises`, by ability id: `by` goes onto the score and `max` onto the most it may be, for good. It is a `"buff"` that `targets` `"self"`, used outside a fight only; a fight never offers it. The raise is kept in the character's live state for the game, so everything the sheet works out reads the raised score. 5e's Manual of Gainful Exercise: `{ "kind": "buff", "targets": "self", "budget": "action", "consumes": true, "raises": { "str": { "by": 2, "max": 2 } } }`.

Conditions picked by the steps paid (Capability API 1.517). An `applies` entry may say `paidSteps` (`atLeast`, `atMost` or both): it goes on only when its entry is paid that many steps above its lowest cost, as `perCostStep` counts them (an innate spell's level above its own counts the same). 5e's Magic Weapon, a 2nd-level spell: `[{ "condition": "magic_weapon_1", "duration": { "rounds": 600 }, "paidSteps": { "atMost": 1 } }, { "condition": "magic_weapon_2", "duration": { "rounds": 600 }, "paidSteps": { "atLeast": 2, "atMost": 3 } }, { "condition": "magic_weapon_3", "duration": { "rounds": 600 }, "paidSteps": { "atLeast": 4 } }]`, each condition with its own `+1`, `+2` or `+3`. Branding Smite's growing 1d6 the same way, a condition for each slot.

Ending somebody else's condition (Capability API 1.518). A `checkEnds`, on an `applies` entry or an item attack's `onHit`, may say `by: "any"`: anybody standing next to its holder (anywhere, in a fight with no board) may spend their own standard budget on the check for them, rolled with their own check, and its holder still may themselves. 5e's alchemist's fire: `{ "condition": "on_fire", "atLeast": 0, "checkEnds": { "check": "agility", "difficulty": 10, "by": "any" } }`.

Further uses that count their own (Capability API 1.519). An item may have up to 16 `moreUses` (8 before 1.519), and each may say `uses`: `max` (1 to 100) and an optional `recharge` (`rests` and `amount`, `"max"` or dice, as `charges.recharge` is). Such a use spends one of its own in place of the item's charges (so it has no `charges` cost of its own), in a fight and outside one, and its count is kept on the item's stack. The inventory shows a button for each further use and what is left of each. 5e's Staff of Thunder and Lightning, each property once a dawn: `{ "label": "Thunderclap", "use": { ... }, "uses": { "max": 1, "recharge": { "rests": ["dawn"], "amount": "max" } } }`; a Helm of Brilliance's 30 diamonds, `"uses": { "max": 30 }` with no recharge.

Wards on a holder (Capability API 1.520). A combat condition may say `wards`, as a zone does (`catalogs`, `maxLevel`, `by`; no `maxLevelPerCostStep`, since nothing casts it): an entry of those catalogs at that level or lower never reaches its holder, from anybody but the holder themselves, and is refused when aimed at them and passes them by in an area. Kept on a creature with `keepsOn`, or on an item's wearer with its `worn.keepsOn`. 5e's rakshasa, Limited Magic Immunity: `{ "condition": "limited_magic_immunity", "effects": [], "wards": { "catalogs": ["spells"], "maxLevel": 6 } }`.

Zones that suppress magic (Capability API 1.521). A zone may say `suppresses`: `catalogs`, `tags` or both. On whoever stands inside it, a condition put on only by entries of those catalogs does nothing (it is not ended, and its clock runs on); an item they hold with any of those tags does nothing while worn or carried, its uses are not offered, and its attacks (made from inside, or at somebody inside) are made as a plain weapon's, without the item's own to-hit and damage bonus, its extra damage, its slaying and those tags as qualities. A zone's `forbids` may also say `casts` now, as a condition's does: nothing of those catalogs is cast from inside it. 5e's Antimagic Field: `"zone": { "rounds": 600, "triggers": [], "suppresses": { "catalogs": ["spells"], "tags": ["magical"] }, "forbids": { "casts": ["spells"] }, "wards": { "catalogs": ["spells"], "maxLevel": 9 }, "inside": { "conditions": ["incapacitated"], "is": { "tags": ["antimagic-susceptible"] } } }`, the last for Antimagic Susceptibility.

Taking a spell in (Capability API 1.522). A combat condition, or an item's `worn`, may say `absorbs`: `catalogs`, `maxLevel` and `by` (as a ward's). An entry of those catalogs at that level or lower, used by a foe with its holder as its only target (never an area), is taken in and does nothing to them; its user's cost is still paid. On a worn item `charges` may say `"spends"` (each one taken in spends its level of the item's charges, and nothing is taken in without enough) or `"gains"` (its level goes onto them, up to their most), so the item holds `charges`. A condition is read first, then worn items in order; an item an antimagic zone suppresses takes in nothing. 5e's Rod of Absorption: `"worn": { "absorbs": { "catalogs": ["spells"], "maxLevel": 9, "charges": "gains" } }` with `"charges": { "max": 50 }`, its stored levels spent through a `use` that restores a slot; Ioun Stone of Absorption, `{ "catalogs": ["spells"], "maxLevel": 4, "charges": "spends" }` with `"charges": { "max": 20 }`.

Turning a spell back (Capability API 1.523). A modifier's `against` may say `"alone": true`: it counts only on a save against something aimed at its holder alone, never an area, and whatever else it names too. A combat condition, or an item's `worn`, may say `turns`: `catalogs`, `maxLevel` and `by` (as a ward's). An entry of those catalogs at that level or lower, used by a foe with its holder as its only target, whose save its holder throws the top face of the die on, does nothing to them and is turned back at its user, made with the same numbers, as though they had been its only target; what it costs is paid once. One turned back is never turned again, and an item an antimagic zone suppresses turns nothing. A fight that throws pools has no top face, so nothing turns there. 5e's Ring of Spell Turning: `"worn": { "modifiers": [{ "to": "saves", "mode": "advantage", "against": { "casts": ["spells"], "alone": true } }], "turns": { "catalogs": ["spells"], "maxLevel": 7 } }`.

Storing a spell to cast later (Capability API 1.524). An item, or a creature, may say `stores`: `catalogs`, `levels` (1 to 40) and `count` (at most 20). In a fight, an entry of one of those catalogs that its user pays for off their sheet may be stored instead of done, in an item of their own (worn or carried) that stores it, in one an ally beside them holds, or in an ally beside them that stores entries itself: its option lists the places under `storesIn` (`holderId`, `item` as its place on the holder's sheet, none for a creature, `label`, and `room`, the levels still free there), and the choice names one as `storesIn` with no targets. It is paid for as it is cast and spends its budget, at the level it is paid at, which must be the first or higher and fit the room; it does nothing now. Whoever wears the item, or the creature itself, may then cast it once, for nothing: an option named after both ("Ring of Spell Storing: Cure Wounds"), at that level, with the save difficulty and attack of whoever stored it, needing none of its components; never while an antimagic zone suppresses the item. What an item holds is kept on its stack after the fight (`storedEntries`, each with `entry`, `label`, `level`, `saveDifficulty` and `toHit`), shown in the inventory, and read as the next fight starts. A creature handed to a fight may carry `stored`, the same list. Set up before a fight, the Game Master's `standing` may name `"into"`: an item of its caster's, or of whom `on` names first, or that creature itself by name, and it is paid for off the caster's sheet at its own level and stored. 5e's Ring of Spell Storing: `"stores": { "catalogs": ["spells"], "levels": 5 }`; the Ioun Stone of Reserve, `{ "catalogs": ["spells"], "levels": 3 }`; the shield guardian's Spell Storing, `{ "catalogs": ["spells"], "levels": 4, "count": 1 }`. A ruleset using it needs Capability API 1.524.

Items forbidden, and more conditions by the slot (Capability API 1.525). A condition's `forbids` may say `"items": { "tags": [...] }`: while it holds, no item with one of those tags is used, in a fight or out of it, nor is an entry stored in one cast; with no `tags`, no item at all. Its weapon blows are still made. 5e's Feeblemind: `"forbids": { "casts": ["spells"], "items": { "tags": ["magical"] } }`. And an entry may put on up to 12 conditions without `appliesOneOf` when each of them says its `paidSteps`, so only those of the steps it is paid at go on: 5e's Branding Smite, a 2d6 to a 9d6 condition, one for each slot from 2nd to 9th. A ruleset using either needs Capability API 1.525.

Saves made as days pass (Capability API 1.526). An entry's `saveEnds` may say `"at": "days"` with `"days": n`: never rolled in a fight, but once the fight that put it on is over, its holder's sheet keeps the save and its difficulty, and each time `n` days of game time pass (the sheet's `pass` command) they make it again, the condition ending on the first success. It counts no `successes` or `failures`. 5e's Feeblemind, "at the end of every 30 days, the creature can repeat its saving throw": `{ "save": "int_save", "at": "days", "days": 30 }` with `"duration": "until-save"`. A ruleset using it needs Capability API 1.526.

A touch answered (Capability API 1.527). A condition's `whenHit` may say `"touches": true`: it then answers, as it answers a hit, whoever tries to grapple or shove its holder, or to break free of their grip, whether that succeeds or not. Its `from` still narrows who it answers, and `within` does not matter, since a touch is made in reach; it goes with no `blow` or with `"blow": "melee"`. 5e's Heated Body, "a creature that touches the azer or hits it with a melee attack while within 5 feet of it takes 5 (1d10) fire damage": `{ "blow": "melee", "within": 5, "touches": true, "harms": { "dice": "1d10", "damageType": "fire" } }`. A ruleset using it needs Capability API 1.527.

Held off while others hold (Capability API 1.528). A combat condition with `heldOffWhile` does nothing while its holder holds one of its `conditions`, or while another condition on them does one of its `effects`. It is not ended, its clock runs on, and it holds again once neither is so. A condition that is itself held off this way never holds off another. 5e's Cloak of Displacement, "suppressed while you are incapacitated, restrained, or otherwise unable to move": `{ "condition": "displaced", "effects": ["attacks-against-disadvantage"], "suppressedWhenHarmed": "until-own-turn-start", "heldOffWhile": { "conditions": ["restrained"], "effects": ["cannot-act", "speed-zero"] } }`. A ruleset using it needs Capability API 1.528.

Walking on water (Capability API 1.529). A combat condition with `"walksOnLiquid": true` lets its holder walk onto water and across it as if it were solid ground, a plain step a cell, and stop there; standing on it they are never under it. Unlike `runsAcross`, it climbs no walls and takes no steps of any height. 5e's Water Walk, and a Ring of Water Walking worn with `keepsOn`: `{ "condition": "water_walking", "effects": [], "walksOnLiquid": true }`. A ruleset using it needs Capability API 1.529.

Target tests with an exception (Capability API 1.530). Wherever a target test (`targetIs`, `from`, `of` and the like) is written, it may say `"except": { ... }`, a test of its own: whoever that one passes never passes this one. With nothing else beside it, anybody else does. 5e's net, "a net has no effect on creatures that are formless": its restrained `applies` with `"targetIs": { "except": { "tags": ["formless"] } }`. A ruleset using it needs Capability API 1.530.

Cut free (Capability API 1.531). A combat condition with `cutFree: { defense, health, damageTypes? }` may be struck at as a thing of its own: an attack that rolls to hit and deals damage of one of `damageTypes` (any type, when left out), made by its holder, or by anybody it would reach them from, friend or foe, is offered against the condition instead. It rolls against `defense`; only damage of those types wears it down, and once `health` of it has, the condition ends on its holder. What is left carries over from one blow to the next. 5e's net, "dealing 5 slashing damage to the net (AC 10) also frees the creature without harming it": `{ "condition": "netted", "effects": ["speed-zero"], "cutFree": { "defense": 10, "health": 5, "damageTypes": ["slashing"] } }`. A ruleset using it needs Capability API 1.531.

Item uses centred on their user (Capability API 1.532). An item's `use`, and each of its `moreUses`, may give its burst `"around": true` (and `"throughWalls": true`), as a catalog entry's area may: never aimed, it catches everybody within its size of whoever uses it, not them. 5e's Staff of Thunder and Lightning, Thunderclap: `"area": { "shape": "burst", "size": 60, "around": true }` with its Constitution save. A ruleset using it needs Capability API 1.532.

Called creatures set aside (Capability API 1.533). A zone's `suppresses` may say `"setsAsideCalled": true` beside its `catalogs`: a creature called into the fight by an entry of one of those catalogs is set aside while the zone covers it. It is out of the fight as one sent away is, takes no turns and is aimed at by nobody, and whatever keeps it (concentration, rounds) runs on; once the zone no longer covers it, moved or ended, it comes back where it stood. 5e's Antimagic Field, "summoned creatures disappear": `"suppresses": { "catalogs": ["spells"], "tags": ["magical"], "setsAsideCalled": true }`. A ruleset using it needs Capability API 1.533.

Weapon hits with their own uses (Capability API 1.534). An entry of an item's `attack.spends` may say `"uses": { "max": 1, "recharge": { "rests": ["long"], "amount": "max" } }` in place of `charges`: it spends one of its own uses, kept on the stack as a further use's are, and comes back on the rests its `recharge` names. Its `amount` may be left out where all it does is its `onHit`. A `save` (`{ "save": "con", "difficulty": 17 }`) the one hit makes keeps its amount off when made, and `drain` (`{ "share": 0.5 }`) gives its wielder that share of what the amount dealt back as health. An `onHit` entry may set `rounds` beside `saveEnds`, the most it lasts, and `"of": "source"` counts its rounds on the wielder's turns. 5e's Rod of Lordly Might, Drain Life: `{ "label": "Drain Life", "uses": { "max": 1, "recharge": { "rests": ["long"], "amount": "max" } }, "amount": { "dice": "4d6" }, "type": "necrotic", "save": { "save": "con_save", "difficulty": 17 }, "drain": { "share": 0.5 } }`; Paralyze: `"onHit": [{ "condition": "paralyzed", "rounds": 10, "save": { "save": "str_save", "difficulty": 17 }, "saveEnds": { "save": "str_save", "difficulty": 17, "at": "turn-end" } }]` with no amount; the Staff of Thunder and Lightning's Thunder: `"onHit": [{ "condition": "stunned", "rounds": 1, "of": "source", "save": { "save": "con_save", "difficulty": 17 } }]`. A ruleset using it needs Capability API 1.534.

Uses beside one another (Capability API 1.535). One of an item's `moreUses` may say `"alongside": ["Thunderclap"]`, the labels of the item's other uses: as it is used, each of those happens too, on whom its burst around its user catches, spending nothing of its own. Only a use centred on its user (`"area": { ..., "around": true }`) may be beside another, so nothing is aimed twice, and one that is has nothing beside it itself. 5e's Staff of Thunder and Lightning, Thunder and Lightning: `{ "label": "Thunder and Lightning", "use": { ...Lightning Strike's line... }, "uses": { "max": 1, "recharge": { "rests": ["long"], "amount": "max" } }, "alongside": ["Thunderclap"] }`, which spends its own daily use and neither of theirs. A ruleset using it needs Capability API 1.535.

Rolled counts (Capability API 1.536). A further use's `uses` may say `"rolled": "1d10"` beside its `max`: each stack of the item has that many, rolled once as it is made and the same every time it is read after, never past `max` (which is then the most the dice can come up). Its `recharge`, where it has one, brings it back to that stack's own count. 5e's Helm of Brilliance: `{ "label": "Daylight (diamond)", "use": { ... }, "uses": { "max": 10, "rolled": "1d10" } }`, and `"max": 30, "rolled": "3d10"` for its fire opals; a Necklace of Prayer Beads' beads, `"max": 6, "rolled": "1d4+2"`. A ruleset using it needs Capability API 1.536.

Calls that grow with the slot (Capability API 1.537). A `calls` may say `"perStep": 2`, that many more creatures for each step of higher cost it is paid with (5e's Animate Dead, "two additional undead creatures for each slot level above 3rd"); an `upTo` may say `"perStep": 1`, its `at` rising that much for each step (Conjure Elemental, "the challenge rating increases by 1 for each slot level above 5th"), so a creature above it is offered and refused unless paid high enough. Each of `options` may say `"fromStep": 2`, offered only when paid that many steps or more, and its own `perStep`, counted past its `fromStep`. 5e's Create Undead from 6th level: `"options": [{ "creature": "creatures/ghoul", "count": { "flat": 3 }, "perStep": 1 }, { "creature": "creatures/ghast", "count": { "flat": 2 }, "fromStep": 2, "perStep": 1 }, { "creature": "creatures/wight", "count": { "flat": 2 }, "fromStep": 2, "perStep": 1 }, { "creature": "creatures/mummy", "count": { "flat": 2 }, "fromStep": 3 }]`. A ruleset using it needs Capability API 1.537.

Called creatures that turn (Capability API 1.538). A `calls` held by its entry's `concentration` may say `"hostileOnBreak": true`: when that concentration is broken (by harm, its caster going down, or a zone that ends it), they do not leave but go over to the other side, out of their caller's command and no longer dismissable; ended any other way, by its caster dropping it or casting another, they leave as before. Their `rounds` still run out. 5e's Conjure Elemental: `"calls": { "from": { ... }, "count": { "flat": 1 }, "rounds": 600, "hostileOnBreak": true }`, "it becomes hostile toward you and your companions, and it might attack". A ruleset using it needs Capability API 1.538.

Summons that strike by themselves (Capability API 1.539). A `summons` may say `"strikesAtTurnStart": true`, with no `budget`: cast, it only appears where it is aimed, and as each of its summoner's turns starts it makes the entry's attack at the nearest creature hostile to them within its `reach` of where it stands (nobody near, nothing), never commanded and never held for an answer. Such a summons may stay up to 14400 `rounds`. 5e's Faithful Hound: `"summons": { "rounds": 4800, "reach": 5, "strikesAtTurnStart": true }` on an entry whose attack is its 4d8 piercing bite with the caster's spell attack. A ruleset using it needs Capability API 1.539.

Creatures that burst as they die (Capability API 1.540). A creature may carry `"onDeath"`, an action written as its `actions` are but never on its menu, whose `area` is a burst `around` it: as it dies (out of the fight, not merely down) it is resolved on whoever the burst catches, its save, damage and `applies` as any action's, never held for an answer. 5e's dust mephit, Death Burst: `"onDeath": { "id": "death-burst", "name": "Death Burst", "budget": "reaction", "area": { "shape": "burst", "size": 5, "around": true }, "save": { "save": "con_save", "difficulty": 10, "onSuccess": "negates" }, "applies": [{ "condition": "blinded", "duration": { "rounds": 1 } }] }`; a balor's Death Throes, 20d6 fire in 30 feet against a Dexterity save of 20 for half. One with a `zone` leaves that area where it fell instead, its cells the burst around every cell it filled; the zone must be `"anchor": "point"` and `"outlasts": true`, so it counts down as each round ends after its maker is gone. A smoke mephit's cloud: `"zone": { "rounds": 10, "anchor": "point", "outlasts": true, "triggers": [], "obscures": {} }`. A ruleset using it needs Capability API 1.540.

Damage that heals a creature instead (Capability API 1.541). A creature's `"healedBy"` lists damage types, matched without case: a blow of one of them deals it nothing (logged as immune) and gives back as many hit points as it would have dealt, never past its maximum. 5e's flesh golem, Lightning Absorption: `"healedBy": ["lightning"]`; a clay golem's acid, an iron golem's fire, a shambling mound's lightning. A ruleset using it needs Capability API 1.541.

Conditions harm puts on a creature (Capability API 1.542). A creature's `"whenHarmedBy"` is up to four rules, each `by` damage types (matched without case) and the `applies` it puts on itself when a blow of one of them deals it anything; a blow it is immune to deals nothing, so puts nothing on. 5e's flesh golem, Aversion of Fire: `"whenHarmedBy": [{ "by": ["fire"], "applies": [{ "condition": "aversion-of-fire", "duration": { "rounds": 1 } }] }]`, a condition giving disadvantage on its attacks and checks to the end of its next turn; a water elemental's Freeze, cold putting on a condition that takes 20 feet off its speed. A ruleset using it needs Capability API 1.542.

A golem's Berserk (Capability API 1.543), in three keys. A creature's `"rollsAtTurnStart"` is up to four rules, each rolled as its turn starts while its health is at most `healthAtMost` and it does not already hold all of its `applies`: `atLeast` or more on `dice` puts them on it. A combat condition's `"behavior": { "nearest": true }` sends its holder, as each of their turns starts, at the nearest other creature they can see, friend or foe: they are walked beside it when it is not, and it is the only one they may attack that turn (seeing nobody, the turn is their own). A combat condition's `"endsAtFullHealth": true` ends it when its holder is healed back to full. 5e's flesh golem: `"rollsAtTurnStart": [{ "healthAtMost": 40, "dice": "1d6", "atLeast": 6, "applies": [{ "condition": "berserk", "duration": "until-dispelled" }] }]`, with `{ "condition": "berserk", "effects": [], "behavior": { "nearest": true }, "endsAtFullHealth": true }`; a clay golem's the same at 60. A ruleset using any of them needs Capability API 1.543.

Strikes at a surprised or not-yet-acted target (Capability API 1.544). A combat condition's change to `attacks` or `damage` may say `"whenTarget": "surprised"` or `"not-yet-acted"` (a target that has not begun a turn in this fight), and a change to `attacks` may say `"critical": true`, making the holder's hit a critical where it reaches. 5e's Assassinate: `[{ "to": "attacks", "mode": "advantage", "whenTarget": "not-yet-acted" }, { "to": "attacks", "critical": true, "whenTarget": "surprised" }]`; a bugbear's Surprise Attack, `{ "to": "damage", "dice": "2d6", "whenTarget": "surprised" }` on a condition it `keepsOn`. A creature's `attackModes` take `whenTarget` too (a doppelganger's Ambusher, advantage on a creature it surprised), and a rider's `when` takes `"target-surprised"` and `"target-not-yet-acted"`. A ruleset using any of them needs Capability API 1.544.

A gnoll's Rampage (Capability API 1.545). A creature's `"rampage": { "action": "bite", "budget": "bonus", "move": 0.5 }` names one of its own actions and one of the fight's budgets: when it drops a creature to 0 with a melee attack on its own turn and has that budget left, the budget is spent, it gets `move` of its speed (half without one) more to move, and the action is free once more this turn. A ruleset using it needs Capability API 1.545.

What follows a charge (Capability API 1.546). A creature action's `charge` may carry `"then": { "action": "bite", "budget": "bonus", "targetHolds": ["prone"] }`: once the charged blow is done on its maker's own turn, if the one it struck holds every condition in `targetHolds`, the named action of the same creature is offered once more this turn, paid from `budget`, at that creature alone. 5e's lion's Pounce and an elk's Charge follow-up. A ruleset using it needs Capability API 1.546.

Actions only in a given shape (Capability API 1.547). A creature action's `"inShape"` lists the shapes it is offered in: `"own"` for its holder's own, or a form's bestiary entry as `"<catalogId>/<entryId>"` (the shape a `shape` condition put it in). A wereboar keeps its Charge with `"inShape": ["creatures/boar", "creatures/hybrid-wereboar"]` (kept across shapes by the shape's `keeps`), its spear with `["own"]`; a mimic's Adhesive in its object form. A ruleset using it needs Capability API 1.547.

Hanging on as one of its forms (Capability API 1.548). A `hangsOn` may carry `"becomes": { "action": "change", "form": "creatures/vampire-mist" }`: as it hangs on at 1, its holder at once takes that form of its own action's `forms`, put on with the action's condition that has a `shape`, exactly as using the action would. 5e's vampire's Misty Escape: `"hangsOn": { "uses": 1, "label": "Misty Escape", "becomes": { "action": "shapechanger", "form": "creatures/vampire-mist" } }`, with a shape condition whose `health` is `"own"`. A ruleset using it needs Capability API 1.548.

Budgets that come back on every turn (Capability API 1.549). A creature may list `"refillsEveryTurn": ["reaction"]` (1 to 4 economy budget ids): whenever any combatant's turn ends, each listed budget is topped back up to its full count, so the creature can spend it again on the next combatant's turn instead of waiting for its own. 5e's marilith's Reactive: `"refillsEveryTurn": ["reaction"]`. An id that is not one of the combat economy's budgets is refused. A ruleset using it needs Capability API 1.549.

Heads that die and grow back (Capability API 1.550). A creature may carry `"heads": { "count": 5, "lostAt": 25, "grows": 2, "growthStoppedBy": ["fire"], "healsPerHead": 10, "opportunityReactions": true, "whileMany": ["many_heads"] }`. It starts with `count` heads (2 to 20). Whenever the damage it takes in a single turn reaches `lostAt`, one head dies, at most one a turn; with no head left it dies. As its own turn ends it grows `grows` heads (default 2) for each that died since its last turn, never past `max` when given, and regains `healsPerHead` hit points for each one grown, unless damage of a type in `growthStoppedBy` (matched without case) reached it since its last turn. With `opportunityReactions`, each head beyond one gives it a spare reaction used only for opportunity attacks, spent before its own reaction and back as its turn starts. While it has more than one head it holds the conditions `whileMany` names, so a condition with save modifiers (`"to": "saves", "mode": "advantage", "against": { "conditions": ["blinded", "charmed", "deafened", "frightened", "stunned", "unconscious"] }`) gives the hydra's save advantage; down to one head it loses them, and gets them back when heads grow. A `sequence` step may carry `"perHead": true`: it strikes `times` for each head the creature has now, and the option asks for that many targets. 5e's hydra's Multiattack: `"sequence": [{ "action": "bite", "perHead": true }]`. A `perHead` step on a creature without `heads` is refused. A ruleset using any of them needs Capability API 1.550.

Weapons that corrode on hitting a creature (Capability API 1.551). A creature may carry `"corrodesWeapons": { "tags": ["metal"], "sparesMagical": true, "ammunition": true }`. A held weapon whose item has any of `tags` that hits it deals its damage, then takes `step` (default 1) off its damage rolls for good: the fight lowers its blows at once, the penalty is written back to the weapon's stack (`corroded`, kept on a stack of one) and read again in every later fight. Once the penalty reaches `destroyedAt` (default 5) the weapon is destroyed: its blows leave the menu and the stack loses it, counted as lost in the journal. `sparesMagical` leaves a weapon tagged `magical` alone. With `ammunition`, ammunition with any of `tags` that hits it is destroyed after dealing damage, so none of that shot is won back after the fight; the launcher itself does not corrode. 5e's rust monster's Rust Metal is `{"tags": ["metal"], "sparesMagical": true, "ammunition": true}`, and a black pudding's Corrosive Form is `{"tags": ["metal", "wood"], "ammunition": true}`. A ruleset using it needs Capability API 1.551.

More conditions (Capability API 1.552). A sheet's `live.conditions`, a rest's `clear.conditions` and `combat.conditions` hold up to 640 entries, up from 320. A ruleset declaring more than 320 needs Capability API 1.552.

Attacks and contests on a target held by its holder (Capability API 1.553). A modifier `to: "attacks"` or `to: "damage"` may carry `"targetHoldsFromSelf": ["grappled"]`: it changes only the holder's own attacks or damage at a target holding one of those conditions that the holder put on them. 5e's Grappler feat is a condition or level with `{"to": "attacks", "mode": "advantage", "targetHoldsFromSelf": ["grappled"]}`. A creature's `attackModes` entry may carry the same key, as a mimic's Grappler does: `{"mode": "advantage", "targetHoldsFromSelf": ["grappled"]}`. A contest with `targetHolds` may carry `"targetHeldBySelf": true`: it is offered only on a target holding that condition from the actor. A contest's `onWin` may carry `"both": { "applies": ["restrained"], "whileTargetHolds": "grappled" }`: each condition goes on the loser and on the winner alike, and each ends as soon as the target no longer holds `whileTargetHolds`, however that ends (an escape, a release, its source going down). The Grappler feat's pin is `{"id": "pin", "label": "Pin", "budget": "action", "attacker": {"checks": ["might"]}, "defender": {"checks": ["might", "agility"]}, "targetHolds": "grappled", "targetHeldBySelf": true, "onWin": {"both": {"applies": ["restrained"], "whileTargetHolds": "grappled"}}}`. A ruleset using any of them needs Capability API 1.553.

Calls raised from a corpse, and held to a most (Capability API 1.554). A creature action's `calls` may carry `"atCorpse": { "withinRounds": 10, "is": { "types": ["humanoid"] } }`: the action is aimed at one creature that died no more than `withinRounds` rounds ago and is `is` (a target test, as `targetIs` reads; any kind when left out), within the action's `reach`, and is offered only while such a corpse lies on the field. The called creature rises in the corpse's space, on its caller's side, and the corpse is gone: it cannot be raised again. A call of `options` cannot be raised from a corpse. `calls` may also carry `"atMost": 7`: the action is not offered while that many creatures it called stand. 5e's wraith's Create Specter is `{"creature": "creatures/specter", "count": {"flat": 1}, "atCorpse": {"withinRounds": 10, "is": {"types": ["humanoid"]}}, "atMost": 7}` with a `reach` of 10 feet. A ruleset using either needs Capability API 1.554.

Walls that give cover, speeds that are the only ones, regeneration kept off somewhere, and fliers, conditions held and alternatives in target tests (Capability API 1.555). A zone's `"cover": "half"` or `"three-quarters"` makes an attack or an effect whose line to its target crosses one of the zone's standing cells (a broken section's cells do not count) against at least that much cover, graded as `combat.cover` grades it; the target's and the attacker's own cells are never between. 5e's Blade Barrier is `"cover": "three-quarters"`. A combat condition's `"onlySpeeds": true` makes its `speeds` its holder's only ways of moving while it holds: their walk and their own other speeds are 0. 5e's Gaseous Form is `{"speeds": {"fly": 10}, "hover": true, "onlySpeeds": true}`. A regeneration's `"notIn": { "light": ["sunlight"], "terrain": ["running-water"] }` keeps it off at the start of a turn its holder begins in one of those lights where they stand, or on a tile or a board whose place `terrain` names (as a creature's `checkModes` read them): a vampire's "if the vampire isn't in sunlight or running water". A target test (`targetIs`, a zone's `keepsOut.is`, and every other place that reads one) may also say `"flying": true` (only one with a fly speed) and `"holding": ["gaseous_form"]` (only one holding one of these combat conditions), each as well as whatever else it asks, and `"anyOf": [ ... ]`, two to four target tests of which one at least must hold. 5e's Wind Wall keeps out `{"anyOf": [{"upToSize": "tiny", "flying": true}, {"holding": ["gaseous_form"]}]}`. A ruleset using any of these needs Capability API 1.555.

Charges and uses that come back after a wait in game time (Capability API 1.556). An item's `charges.recharge`, a further use's `uses.recharge` and a hit's `uses.recharge` may carry `"after": { "days": 2 }` or `"after": { "hours": { "dice": "1d12" } }` (or both, added together): once the count has run out, it waits that much of the game's clock and then gets back its `amount` (all of them for `"max"`). The wait starts the first time the clock moves after the count runs out, and hours that are dice are rolled then; the wait is kept on the stack. With `after`, `rests` may be empty, so no rest brings the count back sooner; with both, either does, and a count given back by a rest waits no more. The clock is the game's own: each time it moves (an action's span, a walk on a map or across the world, days the Game Master's telling passes), the bag's waits count it. 5e's Figurines of Wondrous Power are `{"rests": [], "amount": "max", "after": {"days": 2}}` (each its own days), and Wings of Flying's `{"rests": [], "amount": "max", "after": {"hours": {"dice": "1d12"}}}`. A ruleset using `after` needs Capability API 1.556.

Zones in stages (Capability API 1.557). A zone's `"stages"` (one to ten, in order) say what it does from one of its caster's turns on, counting the turn it was cast as the first: `{ "from": 3, "label": "Lightning", "amount": { "dice": "10d6" }, "damageType": "lightning", "save": "dex_save", "onSave": "half", "count": 6 }`. As its caster's turn starts, the stage that turn is at (the last whose `from` it has reached) strikes whoever is inside with its `amount` of `damageType`, saved against with its `save` at the entry's own difficulty (`onSave` "half", or "none" when left out), and only `count` of them where it says so, its caster's foes first and those nearest its middle first. A stage with no `amount` strikes nobody. While a stage lasts, its `difficult` makes the zone's ground hard going, its `obscures` makes the zone heavily obscuring, and its `noRanged` stops every attack with a ranged weapon into, out of or across it (a spell or an area is no weapon). The entry's own effect is its first turn's, at the zone's `triggers` (which may be empty) or as it is cast. 5e's Storm of Vengeance is its thunder and deafening as cast, then stages from 2 (Acid rain, 1d6 acid), 3 (Lightning, six bolts of 10d6, a Dexterity save for half), 4 (Hail, 2d6 bludgeoning) and 5 (Gusts and freezing rain, 1d6 cold, `difficult`, `obscures`, `noRanged`). A ruleset using `stages` needs Capability API 1.557.

Creatures built from their caller, and acting only as commanded (Capability API 1.558). A catalog entry's or an item use's `calls` may say `"fromCaller": { "health": true, "toHit": <value>, "adds": <value> }`: each creature called has its caller's health maximum as its health; each of its attacks rolls with `toHit` (a value read off the caller's sheet, as `amountAdd` reads one) as its bonus; and `adds`, read the same way, is added to each of its damage amounts. With `"commandedWith": "bonus"` (a budget of the fight's economy) they act only as their caller commands: their first turn, the one they come on, is free, and at each later one their caller pays 1 of that budget or the turn passes (the log says it waited). With `"initiative": "after"`, that turn is right after their caller's, so a caller who spent the budget on their own turn has none left for it. 5e's Arcane Hand calls a Large hand with `{"initiative": "after", "fromCaller": {"health": true, "toHit": <spell attack>, "adds": <spellcasting modifier>}, "commandedWith": "bonus"}`, its four hands its own actions: a Clenched Fist attack, a Forceful Hand's push contest, a Grasping Hand's grapple contest and its crush on whoever it holds, and an Interposing Hand standing in the way as cover. A ruleset using either needs Capability API 1.558.

Rolled tables (Capability API 1.559). A catalog entry's or an item use's `calls` with `"rolled": true` rolls which of its `options` comes as it is used, never picked: each option is as likely as its `"weight"` (1 to 100, 1 unless it says) against the rest, and names one `creature`. An option with `"hostile": true` calls creatures that join the other side, against whoever called them; one with `"none": true` and a `label` calls nobody, only its label told. 5e's Bag of Tricks rolls eight options of one beast each; an Efreeti Bottle rolls `{"creature": "creatures/efreeti", "count": {"flat": 1}, "weight": 1, "hostile": true, "label": "It attacks you"}`, the same efreeti at `"weight": 8` to serve you, and `{"none": true, "weight": 1, "label": "It grants three wishes"}`. An item may also carry a `useTable` of 2 to 24 rows, each a `weight`, a `label` and a `use` of its own, which its `use` rolls on when it says `"rollsTable": true` (a `utility` use aimed at any one creature, or at the side its `targets` names), paying only for itself: the row that comes up happens at whom the use was aimed, centred there when it has an area, or on its user when it targets `self`, and a row with no `use` (grass growing, butterflies) is only told. 5e's Wand of Wonder spends a charge on a use aimed at one creature within 120 feet, and its table rolls among Slow, Faerie Fire, a stun on its user, Stinking Cloud, a rhinoceros's charge, Lightning Bolt, Fireball and the rest. The log says what was rolled and what came up. A ruleset using any of these needs Capability API 1.559.

Double damage to objects (Capability API 1.560). A creature's stat block with `"doubleToObjects": true` deals twice the damage it would to anything that is not a creature: a wall's sections, a layered wall's layers and a board's props, after what the object shrugs off or suffers double. 5e's Siege Monster, on an earth elemental and the other creatures that "deal double damage to objects and structures". A ruleset using it needs Capability API 1.560.

Who understands whom (Capability API 1.561). `combat.languages`, `{"list": <list>, "column": <column>, "prefix": <text>}`, says where a character's languages are read: each row of that sheet list whose column starts with the prefix (when there is one) names a language, the rest of it after the prefix, without regard to case. 5e's is `{"list": "proficiencies", "column": "name", "prefix": "Languages: "}`. A creature's stat block names its own with `"languages": ["Common", "Abyssal"]`. An entry's `targetTypes` with `"understands": true` may only be pointed at, or catch, a creature that understands a language its user does: 5e's Suggestion, Mass Suggestion and Command, which do nothing to a creature that can't understand you. Whoever's languages are not written down (a sheet with no `combat.languages`, a creature with no `languages`) is never kept out by it. What such a spell makes its target do is the conditions' own `behavior` (1.264): Suggestion's options as casting choices (1.313) whose conditions walk away (`moves`), hold still (`only: []`), drop what they hold (`drops`) or waste their action (`wastes`). A ruleset using any of these needs Capability API 1.561.

Harm for defiance (Capability API 1.562). A combat condition's `harms` may come `"at": "attacks-source"`, as its holder makes an attack, or anything that deals damage, at whoever put it on, or `"at": "attacks-source-side"`, at anybody of their side; with `"once": true` it deals that only once while it holds. 5e's Geas, commanding "don't harm me or my friends", is a condition with `{"at": "attacks-source-side", "dice": "5d10", "type": "psychic", "once": true}` (no more than once each day, and a fight is shorter than one). A ruleset using either needs Capability API 1.562.

A heal shared out (Capability API 1.563). A `heal` entry with `"sharesHeal": true` heals one amount, its `amount` (and `amountAdd`) rolled once, shared out among its targets in the order they were picked: each is healed up to their maximum, the next getting what is left, until it runs out. With `"sharesHeal": "half"` nobody is healed past half their maximum, and one already there gets nothing. 5e's Preserve Life is `{"kind": "heal", "amount": {"flat": 0}, "amountAdd": <five times cleric level>, "sharesHeal": "half", "targets": "ally", "targetCount": 6, "range": 30, "targetTypes": {"not": {"types": ["undead", "construct"]}}}`. A ruleset using it needs Capability API 1.563.

Reactions narrowed by what is thrown and who holds what (Capability API 1.564). A reaction's `against` may name `"qualities"`: it answers anything carrying one of them, on a stat block's action or its damage, a catalog entry or an item, whatever the action is called. 5e's Rock Catching answers `{"on": "aimed", "cancels": true, "cancelSave": {...}, "against": {"qualities": ["thrown_object"]}}`, each giant's Rock and a catapult's stone written with `"qualities": ["thrown_object"]` on their damage, its "similar object" included. A moment that answers for others (`for`) may say `"forHolds": [<item tags>]`: it opens only when the one it answers for holds, worn or carried, an item with one of those tags. A shield guardian's Shield is `{"on": "hit", "for": "ally", "within": 5, "at": "target", "forHolds": ["shield_guardian_amulet"]}`, so it guards only whoever wears its amulet. A ruleset using either needs Capability API 1.564.

A contest on a catalog entry, and a target moved where its user picks (Capability API 1.565). An entry's `mechanics.contest` is `{"attacker": [<check ids>], "defender": [<check ids>], "ties": "attacker" | "defender"}`: in place of a roll to hit or a save (it has neither), its user throws the best of `attacker` and each target the best of `defender`, ids of `combat.checks`, and a tie goes to the defender unless it says otherwise. Only on a target it wins does anything else it does land. `mechanics.movesTarget` is `{"distance": <ruleset distance>, "up": true}`: on an entry aimed at one creature, that creature is set down on a cell its user picks, up to `distance` from where it stood and within the entry's `range` of its user, with no step between, so nobody gets a strike at it; with `up` it may be lifted that many levels above the ground there, `levels x levelCells` counted against the distance. Something lifted stays up only while a condition with `hover` holds it; without one it falls as anybody up in the air does. The board asks where once the target is picked, and the player may leave it where it is. 5e's Telekinesis is `{"kind": "debuff", "range": 60, "contest": {"attacker": ["spellcasting"], "defender": ["strength"]}, "movesTarget": {"distance": 30, "up": true}, "applies": [{"condition": "telekinesis_held", "duration": {"rounds": 1}}], "targetTypes": {"is": {"upToSize": "large"}}}`, with `combat.checks` entries `{"id": "spellcasting", "value": {"abilityModFromField": "spellcasting_ability"}}` and `{"id": "strength", "value": {"abilityMod": "str"}}`, and `telekinesis_held` the restrained condition's effects plus `hover`; it is made again each turn with `repeats`, as other concentration spells are. A ruleset using either needs Capability API 1.565.

A condition that lifts its holder (Capability API 1.566). A `combat.conditions` entry may say `"lifts": <ruleset distance>`: while it holds, its holder is up that far above the ground under them, rounded to the board's levels (one at least), and hovers there; when it comes off they fall as anybody up in the air with nothing keeping them there does. Put on by a lasting area's `inside.conditions`, it lifts everyone the area catches and drops them when the area goes. 5e's Reverse Gravity is a zone `{"inside": {"conditions": ["reverse_gravity"], "save": {"save": "dex_save", "ignoresUntilLeaves": true}}}`, the Dexterity save the grab at something fixed, with `{"condition": "reverse_gravity", "effects": [], "lifts": 100}`. A ruleset using it needs Capability API 1.566.

A check mode for finding anybody but whoever put the condition on (Capability API 1.567). A condition's modifier to `"checks"` with a `mode` may say `"exceptSource": true`: it counts only on a Search for the hidden, and there only against each one who did not put the condition on its holder; every other check its holder makes is untouched. 5e's Enthrall is `{"condition": "enthralled", "effects": [], "modifiers": [{"to": "checks", "skills": ["perception"], "mode": "disadvantage", "exceptSource": true}]}`, put on with the spell's save. A ruleset using it needs Capability API 1.567.

Called images rather than creatures (Capability API 1.568). An entry's `calls` may say `"illusion": true`: whatever it calls is an image. Anything aimed at one, or catching one in an area, passes through it and does nothing to it, and the side of whoever did it sees it for what it is from then on: they are never offered it as a target again, while the other side still may be fooled by it. Its caller's own side is never fooled. 5e's Mislead is a self entry whose `applies` makes its caster invisible and whose `calls` is `{"creature": "creatures/illusory-double", "count": {"flat": 1}, "initiative": "after", "commandedWith": "bonus", "illusion": true}`, the double a creature with its caster's speed and a harmless action; Project Image is the same at its own range and duration. A ruleset using it needs Capability API 1.568.

What is stored cast by itself when its moment comes (Capability API 1.569). A `stores` (an item's or a creature's, 1.524) may say `"castsOn": {"on": "harmed" | "down", "at": "self" | "source"}`: the first entry stored in it is cast by itself, for nothing and with its own caster's numbers, as its holder (the wearer of an item) takes damage (`harmed`) or drops to no health still alive (`down`), at them (`self`, the default) or at whoever harmed them (`source`). It goes off once, and is never offered to cast by hand. 5e's Contingency is a worn item (the ivory statuette) with `{"stores": {"catalogs": ["spells"], "levels": 5, "count": 1, "castsOn": {"on": "down"}}}`, its companion spell stored before the fight with `standing`; a shield guardian's block may say `{"catalogs": ["spells"], "levels": 4, "count": 1, "castsOn": {"on": "harmed", "at": "source"}}` for a spell set to go off at whoever strikes it. A ruleset using it needs Capability API 1.569.

An `absorbs` rule can also ask that its holder see whoever cast what it takes in, with `seen: true` (an Ioun Stone of Greater Absorption's "cast by a creature you can see"), and that taking it in cost its holder 1 of an economy budget, with `spends` naming it (a Rod of Absorption's reaction). Without that budget left, or without seeing the caster, the entry lands as usual. `spends` must name one of `combat.economy.budgets`. A ruleset using either needs Capability API 1.570.

A worn item's `absorbs` can also count what it takes in over its whole existence, with `lifetime` (a Rod of Absorption's 50 levels). The count is kept on the inventory stack as `absorbedEver` and written back after each fight. An entry that would take it past `lifetime` lands as usual, and spending its charges never makes room again. Only an item's worn effect may carry it. A ruleset using it needs Capability API 1.571.

A worn item's `paysSlots` names a pool group whose uses its charges may pay for, a charge a level (a Rod of Absorption's "convert energy in the rod into spell slots", `"slots"`). Each use of that group is then also offered paid out of the item at its own pool or any higher one the charges reach, as `payWith` `itemslot:<item>:<pool>`, and casting it that way spends that many charges instead of the pool. The item needs `charges`, and some pool must be of that group. A ruleset using it needs Capability API 1.572.

An item's `charges` may say `"rolled"` dice beside its `max`: each stack is found holding that many rather than full, rolled once for it and the same every time it is read after, from 0 up to `max` (a Rod of Absorption's `{ "max": 50, "rolled": "1d10" }`). Once a fight, a use or a rest changes its count, the stack keeps that count instead. A ruleset using it needs Capability API 1.573.

A charge's `then.action` may name the charging action itself (a warhorse's Trampling Charge, "another attack with its hooves"): it is offered once more from `then.budget` at the same target, and that second blow carries no charge. A ruleset doing so needs Capability API 1.574.

An item's `forms` lets its holder turn it into other forms and back: `label` names it as it was made, `budget` is what turning it spends in a fight, and `into` lists up to 8 forms, each a `label` and another `item` whose `attack`, `worn` effect and `toggle` it takes in that form, keeping everything else of its own (its uses, charges and stores). 5e's Rod of Lordly Might: `{ "label": "Mace", "budget": "bonus", "into": [{ "label": "Flame tongue", "item": "gear/flame_tongue_longsword" }, { "label": "Battering ram", "item": "gear/lordly_ram" }] }`. A fight offers each other form as a switch, the inventory offers them as buttons, and the stack keeps the form it was left in. It is one to a stack, and no form may change form itself. A ruleset using it needs Capability API 1.575.

An item's `stores` may say what it is found holding with `found`: `levels` dice (a Ring of Spell Storing's `"1d6-1"`), rolled once for each stack, and `from`, up to 40 entries of its own catalogs each with the `level` it is held at and an optional `label`. Each stack is filled from that list in an order drawn for it, with each entry that still fits, up to its `count`. The inventory shows it, a fight can cast it, and once a fight stores into it or casts out of it the stack keeps what is left, even nothing. A ruleset using it needs Capability API 1.576.

A condition rule may say `controls: true`: whoever holds it fights on the side of the creature that gave it, and goes back to its own side when the condition ends or its source falls. With `holderDies`, a `save` and a `difficulty` (a value reference), the source makes that save when the creature it controls dies, and falls if it fails, the way Magic Jar's caster dies with a body slain under them. The log says when control is taken and when it is released. A ruleset using it needs Capability API 1.577.

A contest may say `actorHolds`, up to four conditions: it is offered only to a combatant holding every one of them, such as the condition a feat's `keepsOn` gives. The Grappler feat's pin is offered only to whoever has the feat, and with `targetHeldBySelf` only on a creature they grapple. A ruleset using it needs Capability API 1.578.

A zone's `inside.save` may say `"onCast": true`: whoever is inside as the zone is cast throws that save then, before its conditions go on, and on a success ignores what it does inside until they leave, as anybody who makes it later does. 5e's Reverse Gravity: a creature that makes its Dexterity save as the spell is cast grabs something fixed and is never lifted. A ruleset using it needs Capability API 1.579.

A zone's stage may say `"breaksConcentration": true`: while it lasts, whoever starts a turn inside and is concentrating rolls the ruleset's concentration save against the entry's own save difficulty, or 10 where it has none, and loses their concentration on a failure. With `"disperses": true` the zone is a wind while that stage lasts: fog it reaches that is `dispersed` ends, as a zone that `disperses` ends it. 5e's Storm of Vengeance, rounds 5 to 10. A ruleset using it needs Capability API 1.580.

A zone may say `"turnsShots": {}`: no attack from afar with a weapon that is not magical (an attack roll, no area, not a spell, no `magical` quality) is made into, out of or across its cells, as with a storm stage's `noRanged`. Its `spares` names attacks, by label, that go through all the same, up to 12. 5e's Wind Wall, `{"turnsShots": {"spares": ["Rock"]}}` for a giant's hurled boulders. A ruleset using it needs Capability API 1.581.

An entry's `save` may say `"advantageIfFought": true`: a target on a side hostile to its user's in the fight, one they or their companions are fighting, rolls it with advantage. 5e's Charm Person and Enthrall. A ruleset using it needs Capability API 1.582.

An item's `use`, and each of its `moreUses`' `use`, may say `repeats` with `concentration`, as a catalog entry does: while its user concentrates on it, it is offered again on `repeats.budget`, made at the cost it was used at, spending nothing of the item again. 5e's scrolls of Telekinesis, Vampiric Touch, Call Lightning and Heat Metal, and the Staff of the Magi's Telekinesis. A ruleset using it needs Capability API 1.583.

A `combat.conditions` entry may say `endsWhenSource`: it comes off once whoever applied it does one of its `effects` or holds one of its `conditions`, once something stops them using one of its `components` (as Silence stops `verbal`), or once its holder lacks one of its `holderLacks` senses (a declared combat sense, or one a condition's `lacks` names, as 5e's deafened names `hearing`). 5e's Enthrall, which ends if its caster is incapacitated or can no longer speak and holds only those who can hear them: `{"effects": ["cannot-act"], "components": ["verbal"], "holderLacks": ["hearing"]}`. A ruleset using it needs Capability API 1.584.

A `combat.conditions` entry may say `countsAs`, up to four other conditions it counts as: an immunity to one of them keeps it off (`conditionImmunities` and `conditionImmunitiesFrom`), what `suppresses` one holds it off too, a save against one is a save against it (so advantage on saves against being charmed reaches it), and an `ends` that names one ends it too. 5e's Enthrall and Geas, `"countsAs": ["charmed"]`. A ruleset using it needs Capability API 1.585.

A creature action may say `"callerAdds": false`: on a creature called with `fromCaller.adds`, that action's damage takes nothing from its caller. With `perCallerStep`, an amount (dice the size of the action's own damage dice, a flat amount, or both), it deals that much more for each step the call was paid above its base. 5e's Arcane Hand's Clenched Fist, `{"damage": {"dice": "4d8", "type": "force"}, "callerAdds": false, "perCallerStep": {"dice": "2d8"}}`. A ruleset using it needs Capability API 1.586.

A contest may say `advantageAtMostSize`, a size of `combat.sizes`: its actor rolls with advantage at a target no bigger than that. Its `onWin` may say `pushRead`, a value read off the actor's sheet, or its caller's for a called creature with none of its own, in the ruleset's distance, added to `push`. 5e's Arcane Hand's Forceful Hand is a contest only the hand makes (`actorHolds` a condition its block `keepsOn`), with `"advantageAtMostSize": "medium"` and `{"onWin": {"push": 5, "pushRead": <five times the spellcasting modifier>}}`; its Grasping Hand is another such contest, its grapple made with the hand's own Strength. A ruleset using it needs Capability API 1.587.

A combat condition may say `coverFor` (`{"tier": "half" | "three-quarters", "who"?: "source" | "caller"}`): its holder's attacks at whoever applied it, or with `"who": "caller"` at the creature that called the applier, meet at least that cover, and so do saves that one throws against the holder's effects where `cover.saves` names them, while the applier stands. 5e's Arcane Hand's Interposing Hand applies a condition with `{"tier": "half", "who": "caller"}` to the creature it stands in front of. It needs `combat.cover`. A ruleset using it needs Capability API 1.588.

`combat.languages` may say `split`, up to four separators: each row's text after the prefix is split on any of them into several languages, so with 5e's `[", ", " and "]` a race's one row "Languages: Common and Dwarvish" names two. A character whose sheet has no row naming a language now has their languages unwritten, keeping nobody out, as a stat block without `languages` does. A ruleset using `split` needs Capability API 1.589.

A worn item's `paysSlots` now pays only for pools its holder has some of (a `max` above 0 on their sheet), and it may say `paysSlotsMax`, a pool of that group: none past it is offered. 5e's Rod of Absorption, "a level equal to or lower than your own spell slots, up to a maximum of 5th level", is `{"paysSlots": "slots", "paysSlotsMax": "slots_5"}`. A ruleset using `paysSlotsMax` needs Capability API 1.590.

A `rolled` call's option may say `rounds` (those it calls leave after that many, in place of the call's own), `spendsItem` (once it comes, the item it was rolled from is spent for good, its uses never offered again), or `pastLifetime`. On an item's use the call may say `keepsRoll` (its first roll is kept on the item and the same option comes at every later use) and `lifetimeUses` (after that many uses over the item's existence, each further use is its one `pastLifetime` option, never rolled). What it kept, how often it was used and whether it is spent stay on the item after the fight. 5e's Efreeti Bottle: `{"rolled": true, "keepsRoll": true, "lifetimeUses": 3, "options": [{"creature": "...", "weight": 1, "hostile": true, "rounds": 5, "spendsItem": true}, {"creature": "...", "weight": 8}, {"none": true, "weight": 1, "label": "Three wishes", "spendsItem": true}, {"none": true, "label": "It escapes", "pastLifetime": true, "spendsItem": true}]}`. A ruleset using any of these needs Capability API 1.591.

A catalog entry's or item use's `save` may say `failedBy` (`{"atLeast": 5, "applies": [...]}`), as an aura's save has since 1.268: a failure by at least that much puts those on in place of the entry's `applies`. 5e's Wand of Wonder, petrified outright on a failure by 5 or more and restrained on any other: `{"applies": [{"condition": "restrained"}], "save": {"save": "con_save", "onSuccess": "negates", "failedBy": {"atLeast": 5, "applies": [{"condition": "petrified"}]}}}`. A ruleset using it needs Capability API 1.592.

A catalog entry's or item use's mechanics may say `amountTimes` (2 to 100), its `amount`'s roll multiplied by it, and `"dividedAmong": "caught"`, that total split evenly, rounded down, among the creatures its `area` catches, with no attack roll or save. 5e's Wand of Wonder gems, "1d4 × 10 gems ... the total damage of the gems is divided equally among all creatures in the line": `{"area": {"shape": "line", "size": 30, "width": 5}, "amount": {"dice": "1d4"}, "amountTimes": 10, "dividedAmong": "caught", "damageType": "bludgeoning"}`. A ruleset using either needs Capability API 1.593.

A catalog entry's mechanics may say `targetsWithinFirst`, a distance: each creature picked after the first must stand within it of the first one picked, where `targetsWithin` holds every pair to it (an entry takes one of the two). 5e's Chain Lightning, "three other targets, each of which must be within 30 feet of the first target": `{"targetCount": 4, "targetsWithinFirst": 30}`, the first creature named its first target. Two leaps 50 feet apart on either side of the first are allowed; one 35 feet from the first is refused as standing too far. A ruleset using it needs Capability API 1.594.

A zone's `keepsOut` may say `"reach": true`: those it keeps out do not reach through it either. One of them outside cannot make a melee attack with no more than the least reach (one cell) at whoever stands inside, nor strike them in passing as they move inside it; an attack from afar, a weapon with longer reach and a spell still go through. With `"endsOnSweep": true`, a zone around its caster (`anchor: "caster"`) or carried ends as soon as whoever it is around moves so that it covers one it keeps out (logged as ended). 5e's Antilife Shell, "prevents an affected creature from passing or reaching through ... If you move so that an affected creature is forced to pass through the barrier, the spell ends": `{"area": {"shape": "burst", "size": 10}, "zone": {"anchor": "caster", "keepsOut": {"is": {"except": {"types": ["undead", "construct"]}}, "reach": true, "endsOnSweep": true}, ...}}`. A ruleset using either needs Capability API 1.595.

A combat condition's `hangsOn` may say `"ends": true`: once it works, the condition it is on is over (a creature's or a level's is refused). With `"outright": true` it works as well against what would slay its holder outright without dealing damage: an entry's `kills`, its `killsAtMost`, a failed save that slays, or a tier that destroys. That is undone, nothing of it taking, and the log says they were spared being slain outright; it counts as a use, a pool spent, as hanging on at 1 does. It reads no blow, so it goes without `save`, `notFrom`, `notCritical` and `dealtAtMost`. 5e's Death Ward, "the first time the target would drop to 0 hit points as a result of taking damage, the target instead drops to 1 hit point, and the spell ends. If the spell is still in effect when the target is subjected to an effect that would kill it instantaneously without dealing damage, that effect is instead negated against the target, and the spell ends": the condition's `{"hangsOn": {"uses": 1, "ends": true, "outright": true, "label": "Death Ward"}}`. A ruleset using either needs Capability API 1.596.

More contests (Capability API 1.597). `combat.contests` holds up to 16 entries, up from 12. A ruleset declaring more than 12 needs Capability API 1.597.

A contest's `onWin` may say `"follows": true` alongside `push`: the winner moves with the one it pushed, into the last cell they left on the way, so it stays beside them; one wider than a cell, or with no room there, moves to the open place beside them nearest where it stood. It draws no strikes, as the push draws none, and with no room there it stays where it is. 5e's Arcane Hand's Forceful Hand, "the hand moves with the target to remain within 5 feet of it": `{"onWin": {"push": 5, "pushRead": {...}, "follows": true}}`. A ruleset using it needs Capability API 1.598.

A combat condition's `endsWhenSource` may say `"acts": true`: it comes off once whoever applied it takes another action, anything but a move, a reaction or ending their turn. Using the same action again puts it on afresh, where that action says. 5e's Arcane Hand's Interposing Hand, "until you give the hand a different command": `{"condition": "interposed", "endsWhenSource": {"acts": true}, "coverFor": {...}}`. A ruleset using it needs Capability API 1.599.

A creature may say `passableBy` (`{"ability": "str", "above": 26, "difficult": true}`): a foe moves through its space only with that ability score above `above`, and with `difficult` its space is hard going to them, each cell costing double. Without it, a foe is let through only as sizes allow. `"fillsSpace": false` says it fills none of its space: a friend may stop in it, and so may a foe it lets through; something pushed, set down or moved beside somebody may end there too. 5e's Arcane Hand, "The target can't move through the hand's space if its Strength score is less than or equal to the hand's Strength score.
