If its Strength score is higher ... that space is difficult terrain for the target ... The hand doesn't fill its space": `{"passableBy": {"ability": "str", "above": 26, "difficult": true}, "fillsSpace": false}`. A ruleset using either needs Capability API 1.600.

A combat condition's `behavior` may say `"actions": true` alongside `only`: the condition's own `actions` stay offered too, where `only` would filter them out with everything else. 5e's Wind Walk, "The only actions a creature can take in this form are the Dash action or to revert to its normal form": `{"condition": "wind_walking", "actions": ["spells/wind-walk-revert"], "behavior": {"only": ["move", "dash"], "actions": true}}`. It needs `only` and actions of the condition's own. From 1.601 a `utility` entry whose only effect is `endsCondition` is offered as an action too, so the revert can be `{"kind": "utility", "targets": "self", "endsCondition": "wind_walking"}`. A ruleset using it needs Capability API 1.601.

A wall's `area`, a catalog entry's or an item use's, may say `"ringOnly": true` beside its `ring`: it is always laid as that ring, never as a straight wall. And a zone's `blocks` may name `"spells"`, on a wall or on any other zone: no spell is cast into, out of or across its cells. A spell aimed at somebody on its other side is refused as out of line, an area spell is not aimed into it, and one aimed beside it stops at its edge. Weapons are not stopped, and a spell cast from inside at somebody else inside is not either. A zone that only blocks spells closes no cell to walking or sight and needs no trigger. 5e's Forcecage, as a cage `{"area": {"shape": "wall", "size": 80, "ring": 20, "ringOnly": true}, "zone": {"blocks": ["move"]}}` and as a box `{"area": {"shape": "box", "size": 20}, "zone": {"blocks": ["spells"]}}`. A ruleset using either needs Capability API 1.602.

A zone's `forbids.teleport` may be `{"save": "<save id>", "difficulty": "caster"}` in place of `true`: then a creature inside it that teleports out first throws that save against the difficulty of the entry that made the zone. Made, it goes. Failed, it stays where it is, the log says so, and the use is spent all the same (nobody it would have carried goes either). Each creature carried out with someone else saves for itself. Teleporting within the zone or into it is not stopped, and neither is leaving the fight by teleport once the save is made. A wall laid as a ring holds every cell it rings, so the middle of a ring counts as inside. 5e's Forcecage: `{"forbids": {"teleport": {"save": "cha_save", "difficulty": "caster"}}}`. A ruleset using it needs Capability API 1.603.

An entry's `repeats` may say `"afterTargetDown": true`: the repeat is offered only once everybody the cast was last aimed at is down (at 0 hit points or out of the fight), still at no cost. Made, the conditions the entry's `applies` put on those creatures come off them and go on the new target, which is then the one it waits on next. It needs `applies`, and it is not used with `sameTargets`. 5e's Hunter's Mark, "If the target drops to 0 hit points before this spell ends, you can use a bonus action on a subsequent turn of yours to mark a new creature": `{"repeats": {"budget": "bonus", "afterTargetDown": true}}`. A ruleset using it needs Capability API 1.604.

A combat condition may say `"heldOnlyIn": {"light": [...]}` with any of `sunlight`, `bright`, `dim` and `dark`: it holds only while its holder stands in one of those lights, read where they stand as `checkModes.light` is. Anywhere else it is held off, not ended, and it holds again once they are back in that light. 5e's shadow's Sunlight Weakness, a kept-on condition with `own-attacks-disadvantage`, `own-checks-disadvantage` and `own-saves-disadvantage`: `{"heldOnlyIn": {"light": ["sunlight"]}}`. A ruleset using it needs Capability API 1.605.

A creature's action may say `"free": true` and `"beforeAttack": true`, as a catalog entry may (1.247): it costs no budget, and it is offered only on the creature's own turn before it has made an attack in it. When the Engine runs the creature, it takes a free `beforeAttack` action that lands on itself (`self`) just before it makes a melee weapon attack, and never in place of one. 5e's berserker's and minotaur's Reckless: `{"id": "reckless", "name": "Reckless", "budget": "action", "free": true, "beforeAttack": true, "self": true, "applies": [{"condition": "reckless", "duration": {"rounds": 1}}, {"condition": "exposed", "duration": {"rounds": 1, "at": "turn-start"}}]}`. A ruleset using either needs Capability API 1.606.

A creature's `move` may say `"toward": "seen-hostile"`: every step of it must bring the creature nearer a hostile creature it can see, and seeing none, it goes nowhere. Such a move needs no `signature` when its action does nothing but move: it is then taken on the creature's own turn with the action's `budget`, picked on the board as a walk is, and the turn's own movement is kept. 5e's orc's Aggressive: `{"id": "aggressive", "name": "Aggressive", "budget": "bonus", "move": {"toward": "seen-hostile"}}`. The Engine-run creature weighs it as it weighs a legendary move. A ruleset using it needs Capability API 1.607.

`endsWhenHolder` may say `"incapacitated": true`, on a combat condition or on what an entry `applies`: the condition comes off once its holder holds anything with `cannot-act`. And a condition's aura may say `"seenByHolder": true`: it reaches only whoever its holder can see, besides the holder itself. An aura's `targetTypes.understands` (1.561) reads the holder's own languages. 5e's knight's Leadership, a self-applied ten-round condition: `{"condition": "leadership", "endsWhenHolder": {"incapacitated": true}, "auras": [{"distance": 30, "side": "allies", "self": true, "needs": ["hearing"], "seenByHolder": true, "targetTypes": {"understands": true}, "conditions": ["led"]}]}`. A ruleset using either needs Capability API 1.608.

A zone's `obscures` may say `"notForCaster": true`: it obscures for everybody but its caster, who sees through it whatever their senses. 5e's Staff of Swarming Insects' Insect Cloud, a 30-foot radius anchored on its caster: `{"zone": {"anchor": "caster", "obscures": {"dispersed": true, "notForCaster": true}}}`. A ruleset using it needs Capability API 1.609.

A call (a catalog entry's, an item's or a creature action's) may say `"leash": <distance>`: each creature it called leaves once its caller is farther from it than that. A creature action's `calls.dismissible` may be `{"budget": "<id>"}`, its caller sending them away on that budget instead of the action's own. 5e's treant's Animate Trees, the trees leaving on a bonus action or once the treant is more than 120 feet off: `{"calls": {"creature": "monsters/animated_tree", "count": {"flat": 2}, "endsWithCaller": true, "dismissible": {"budget": "bonus"}, "leash": 120, "rounds": 14400}}`. A ruleset using either needs Capability API 1.610.

A catalog entry's or an item's `calls` may say `"hostileUnless": {"derived": "<id>", "atLeast": N}`: unless its user's derived value is at least N, those it calls come on the other side, against their user, as a rolled call's `hostile` option does. 5e's brass Horn of Valhalla, needing proficiency with all simple weapons: `{"calls": {"creature": "monsters/berserker", "count": {"dice": "3d4"}, "rounds": 600, "hostileUnless": {"derived": "proficient_simple_weapons", "atLeast": 1}}}`. A ruleset using it needs Capability API 1.611.

An item's `calls` may say `"spendsItemOnDeath": true`: once a creature it called dies, the item is spent for good, as a rolled option's `spendsItem` is. And an item's `recharge.after` (on its charges or a further use's uses) may say `"fromDeparture": true`: the wait starts only once the creature its use called has left, the rest of its `rounds` past the end of a fight counted as game time with the fight's new `combat.roundSeconds` (how long a round lasts, in seconds; 5e's 6), which `fromDeparture` needs. 5e's Ring of Djinni Summoning, once a day and gone for good with its djinni: `{"combat": {"roundSeconds": 6}}` and `{"charges": {"max": 1, "recharge": {"rests": [], "amount": "max", "after": {"hours": {"flat": 24}, "fromDeparture": true}}}, "use": {"calls": {"creature": "monsters/djinni", "count": {"flat": 1}, "rounds": 600, "spendsItemOnDeath": true}}}`. A ruleset using any of the three needs Capability API 1.612.

An item's `calls` may say `"becomesItem": true`: the item itself is the creature it calls, out of its holder's hands while that creature stands, so none of its uses or blows is offered (and it calls no second one). And a catalog entry's or an item's `calls` may say `"dismissible": true`, as a creature action's may: its user has an action, on the entry's or the use's own budget or with `{"budget": "<id>"}` on that one, that sends them away again. 5e's Staff of the Python, a snake on its own initiative turned back on a bonus action, and shattered if the snake dies: `{"calls": {"creature": "monsters/giant_constrictor_snake", "count": {"flat": 1}, "becomesItem": true, "dismissible": {"budget": "bonus"}, "spendsItemOnDeath": true}}`. A snake turned back before it fell regains all its hit points, as every call is a fresh creature. A ruleset using either needs Capability API 1.613.

A creature action's `calls`, or any one of a call's `options`, may say `"onlyWhere"`: it is offered only where its caller stands in none of the lights in `"notLight"` (read as `regenerates.notIn` reads light), and with `"outdoors": true` (or `"indoors": true`) only where the board's place is outdoors (or indoors). Dungeons, caves, castles, spaceships and mansions are indoors; every other place, and a board that names none, is outdoors. And a creature action's `calls` may say `"arrivesIn": {"dice": "1d4"}`: those it calls appear only once that many of their caller's turns have started, rolled as they are called. 5e's vampire's Children of the Night: `{"calls": {"options": [{"creature": "monsters/swarm_of_bats", "count": {"dice": "2d4"}, "onlyWhere": {"notLight": ["sunlight"]}}, {"creature": "monsters/swarm_of_rats", "count": {"dice": "2d4"}, "onlyWhere": {"notLight": ["sunlight"]}}, {"creature": "monsters/wolf", "count": {"dice": "3d6"}, "onlyWhere": {"notLight": ["sunlight"], "outdoors": true}}], "arrivesIn": {"dice": "1d4"}, "rounds": 600}}`. A ruleset using either needs Capability API 1.614.

A catalog entry's or an item's `calls` may say `"side": "none"`: those it calls are under nobody's control. Each of their turns they only walk toward their caller by the shortest open way, and they keep no side standing. With `"tamedWhile": "<condition>"`, one that holds that condition is its caller's to command, as any called creature is, for as long as it holds it. 5e's Pipes of the Sewers takes three uses. The first is a bonus action spending 1 to 3 charges, one swarm a charge: `{"charges": 1, "chargeStep": {"max": 2}, "calls": {"creature": "monsters/swarm_of_rats", "count": {"flat": 1}, "perStep": 1, "side": "none", "tamedWhile": "piped"}}`. The second is the playing, a contest (1.565) won over a swarm it called (`targetTypes.calledBySelf`, 1.487) within 30 feet, putting on `piped` for a round: `{"contest": {"attacker": ["cha"], "defender": ["wis"]}, "applies": [{"condition": "piped", "rounds": 1}]}`. The third is keeping it up with an action each round (`sustains`, 1.431). A ruleset using `side` or `tamedWhile` needs Capability API 1.615.

An entry's `applies[].duration` may say `"remainingOf": "<condition>"` beside its `rounds`: the condition lasts no longer than what was left of that one on its target when it last came off before running out, counted down a round each round since, and `rounds` at most. The entry is offered only while some of it is left and its user is under neither condition. 5e's Wind Walk, back into the cloud for what is left of the spell's 8 hours, a minute to do: `{"targets": "self", "castingRounds": 10, "applies": [{"condition": "wind_walking", "duration": {"rounds": 4800, "remainingOf": "wind_walking"}}]}`. A ruleset using it needs Capability API 1.616.

A catalog entry's mechanics, an item's `use` or a further use may say `"userHolds": ["<condition>", ...]`: it is offered only while its user holds one of those conditions. And a combat condition's `endsWhenSource` may say `"lacks": ["<condition>", ...]`: it comes off once whoever put it on holds none of them. 5e's Pipes of the Sewers, a "Play" use that puts a one-round `piping` on its user, a call and a sway with `"userHolds": ["piping"]`, and the sway's `piped` with `{"endsWhenSource": {"lacks": ["piping"]}}`. A ruleset using either needs Capability API 1.617.

An entry's or an item use's `contest` (1.565) may say `"onLose": {"applies": [{"condition": "<id>", "rounds": N}, ...]}`: on a target its user loses to, those conditions go on, with the user as their source, each for its rounds; a `targetTypes.not.holding` naming one keeps that target out after. And a combat condition's `endsWhenHolder` may say `"cantHearSource": {"sense": "<sense>"}`, with `"beyond": <distance>` when it says: it comes off as its holder starts a turn lacking that sense, farther than `beyond` from whoever put it on, or with them gone or down; its `onEnd` goes on as it does. 5e's Pipes of the Sewers: the sway's `{"contest": {..., "onLose": {"applies": [{"condition": "pipes_spurned", "rounds": 14400}]}}, "targetTypes": {"not": {"holding": ["pipes_spurned"]}}}`, and `piped` with `{"endsWhenHolder": {"cantHearSource": {"sense": "hearing"}}, "onEnd": {"applies": [{"condition": "pipes_spurned", "duration": {"rounds": 14400}}]}}`. A ruleset using either needs Capability API 1.618.

What an item does while worn (or carried, or while its wearer falls short of what it requires) may say `"ignoredBy": {"entries": ["<catalog>/<entry>", ...]}`: a creature made from one of those bestiary entries never aims at its wearer, nor does the Engine run it at them. With `"untilHarmed": true`, one the wearer attacks, harms, contests or forces a save on aims at them again for the rest of the fight. 5e's Pipes of the Sewers, worn while attuned: `{"worn": {"ignoredBy": {"entries": ["creatures/rat", "creatures/giant-rat", "creatures/swarm-of-rats"], "untilHarmed": true}}}`. A ruleset using it needs Capability API 1.619.

A catalog entry's or an item's `calls` may say `"commandedWithin": <distance>`: as each creature it called starts a turn, its caller commands it only while standing, able to act (holding nothing with `cannot-act`) and, on a board, within that distance of it. Otherwise it acts on its own that turn, run by the Engine even where the player took it, defending itself and going at its caller's foes, and with `commandedWith` its caller pays nothing for that turn. 5e's Staff of the Python: `{"calls": {"creature": "creatures/giant-constrictor-snake", "commandedWith": "bonus", "commandedWithin": 60}}`. A ruleset using it needs Capability API 1.620.

A call (a creature action's, a catalog entry's or an item's) may say `"within": {"distance": <distance>}`, with `"seen": true` when it says: those it calls are set down on open cells within that distance of their caller, only ones the caller can see with `seen`, the ones nearest the caller's foes first. Off a board, it changes nothing. 5e's treant's Animate Trees: `{"calls": {"creature": "creatures/animated-tree", "count": {"flat": 2}, "leash": 120, "within": {"distance": 60, "seen": true}}}`. A ruleset using it needs Capability API 1.621.

A combat condition may say `"endable": {"budget": "<budget>"}`: while they hold it, its holder is offered "End" with its label, which takes it off for 1 of that budget, as an entry's `endsCondition` does (concentration that holds it ends with it). 5e's Ring of Invisibility, `{"condition": "ring_invisible", "effects": [...], "endable": {"budget": "bonus"}}`, the Potion of Gaseous Form and the Boots of Speed. A ruleset using it needs Capability API 1.622.

An item's `use` or further use may say `"chargesPerRound": true`: what it puts on lasts while the item's charges do. The use pays its own `charges` as ever, for its first round; each round its condition then holds, as that condition's clock counts a round down, spends one more, and it comes off as the last is spent. Ended sooner (by `endable`, say), the rest are kept for the next use. 5e's Boots of Speed, an item with `"charges": 100` (10 minutes in rounds) and a use `{"kind": "buff", "targets": "self", "budget": "bonus", "charges": 1, "chargesPerRound": true, "applies": [{"condition": "boots_of_speed", "duration": {"rounds": 100}}]}`. A ruleset using it needs Capability API 1.623.

An item's `charges` may say `"overuse": {"chance": <1-100>, "cumulative": true}`: once they are all spent, its uses are still offered, and each use with none left rolls a d100. At or under `chance` the item tears apart, gone for good, and the use does nothing; with `cumulative` the chance is that much again for each such use since its charges last came back (20, then 40, then 60). The count is kept on its stack and starts over when a rest or a wait brings charges back. 5e's Wind Fan, `{"max": 1, "recharge": {"rests": ["long"], "amount": "max"}, "overuse": {"chance": 20, "cumulative": true}}`. A ruleset using it needs Capability API 1.624.

A duration of rounds may say `"times": "<dice>"`: it lasts its `rounds` that many times over, rolled as it is put on. 5e's Potion of Growth, "1d4 hours", is `{"rounds": 600, "times": "1d4"}`. The most the dice can roll, times `rounds`, is no more than a day (14400 rounds). A ruleset using it needs Capability API 1.625.

An entry or an item's use taken at a `"caught"` moment may say `"keepsCaught": true`, or `{"otherwise": "<catalog>/<entry>"}`: its holder keeps the missile they just caught. Where it was one of its shooter's own ammunition, that is the item kept, and the shooter no longer wins it back after the fight; otherwise (a creature's own arrow) it is the `otherwise` item, and with none named nothing is kept. It goes onto a stack of the same item in the catcher's bag, or a new one beside their others, written back with the fight's other items. It deals nothing of its own; with `"free": true` it takes no reaction, being part of the one that cut the missile to nothing. 5e's Gloves of Missile Snaring, `{"kind": "utility", "targets": "self", "free": true, "reaction": {"on": "caught", "freeSlot": "hand"}, "keepsCaught": {"otherwise": "gear/arrow"}}`. A ruleset using it needs Capability API 1.626.

An entry or an item's use that `dismisses` its own user while a condition it puts on them holds, with `"turns": true`, sends them out of the fight still taking their turns; where that condition is `endable`, its "End" is offered on those turns, and taking it brings them back where they left, or the nearest free place. 5e's Robe of Stars, "use an action to enter the Astral Plane ... until you use an action to return": `{"kind": "buff", "targets": "self", "budget": "action", "applies": [{"condition": "astral", "duration": "until-dispelled"}], "dismisses": {"while": "astral", "turns": true}}` with the combat condition `{"condition": "astral", "effects": [], "endable": {"budget": "action"}}`. A ruleset using it needs Capability API 1.627.

An item's `use` (or a further use) may say `needs` and `needsAny`, as a catalog entry does: only a creature with those senses is affected. And `oncePerTarget` may go on an entry or use with an `area`: whoever it was spent on already since the rest that frees it is passed over by the area, and with `whenSaved` only those who made their save are marked. 5e's Pipes of Haunting, `{"kind": "debuff", "area": {"shape": "burst", "size": 30, "around": true}, "friendlyFire": false, "needs": ["hearing"], "save": {...}, "applies": [...], "oncePerTarget": {"until": "long", "whenSaved": true}}`: only hostile creatures that hear it, and one that saved is immune until a long rest. (`friendlyFire: false` already spares its user's own side.) A ruleset using either needs Capability API 1.628.

An entry or item use that deals damage may say `amountAgainst: {"is": {...}, "amount": {"dice": "10d6"}}`: a target that is what `is` names takes that amount instead of the entry's own (and at its most, when `maxAgainst` names it too). With `save.modes` (1.185) it rolls its save at disadvantage as well. 5e's Horn of Blasting, `{"kind": "attack", "amount": {"dice": "5d6"}, "damageType": "thunder", "save": {"save": "con_save", "onSuccess": "half", "modes": [{"is": {"tags": ["glass", "crystal"]}, "mode": "disadvantage"}]}, "amountAgainst": {"is": {"tags": ["glass", "crystal"]}, "amount": {"dice": "10d6"}}}`. A ruleset using it needs Capability API 1.629.

An item's `use` (or a further use) may say `backfires: {"chance": 20, "amount": {"dice": "10d6"}, "damageType": "fire", "destroys": true}`: each time it is used, a d100 at or under `chance` deals `amount` to its user, and with `destroys` the item is gone for good. The use still works unless it says `instead: true`, when the backfire is all that happens. 5e's Horn of Blasting explodes this way. A ruleset using it needs Capability API 1.630.

An entry or item use with an `area` and a `save` may say `pushesOutSaved: true`: whoever it catches and makes their save is pushed straight away from where it was aimed (its user, for a burst around them) until no cell they fill is inside it, as far as the way allows. 5e's Bead of Force, a 10-foot sphere whose savers "are pushed away from the center of the sphere until they are no longer inside it", while those who fail are sealed inside (`applies` a condition with `sealed`). A creature sealed inside rolling the sphere at half its speed is the sealing condition's `"sealed": {"rolls": {"budget": "action", "share": 0.5}}` (1.314). A ruleset using it needs Capability API 1.631.

A combat condition's `onEnd` may say `when`, the ways it must have ended for what it leaves behind to happen: `expired`, `save`, `damage`, `harmed`, `concentration`, `spent`, `cured`, `dispelled`, `contest`, `freed`, `slipped`, `endsWhenSource` (its source holding, lacking or being stopped by what `endsWhenSource` names) or `cantHearSource` (`endsWhenHolder.cantHearSource`). Ended any other way, it leaves nothing. 5e's Pipes of the Sewers: a swayed swarm's `piped` ends when its piper stops playing or when it can't hear them, but only the second leaves it unswayable for a day, `"onEnd": {"when": ["cantHearSource"], "applies": [{"condition": "pipes_spurned", "duration": {"rounds": 14400}}]}`. A ruleset using it needs Capability API 1.632.

`targetTypes` may say `uncontrolled: true`: only a creature under no other creature's control may be picked or caught, that is one nobody called into the fight, one its user called, or one called but wild (`calls.wild`) or turned on its caller. 5e's Pipes of the Sewers sway "a swarm of rats that isn't under another creature's control", `"targetTypes": {"is": {"tags": ["swarm"]}, "uncontrolled": true}`: a wild swarm or the piper's own, never a vampire's. A ruleset using it needs Capability API 1.633.

A combat condition's `actions` may name an entry as `{"entry": "spells/animal-friendship", "saveDifficulty": 13}` (and `toHit`), used at those numbers rather than its holder's own spellcasting. 5e's Potion of Animal Friendship: whoever drinks it may cast animal friendship at will for an hour at save DC 13, `{"condition": "animal_friend", "effects": [], "actions": [{"entry": "spells/animal-friendship", "saveDifficulty": 13}]}`. A ruleset using it needs Capability API 1.634.

A combat condition's `harms.save` may say `drops`, dice of the size it harms with: each save its holder makes takes them off its harm for good, and once none are left the condition ends. 5e's Potion of Poison, `"harms": {"at": "turn-start", "dice": "3d6", "type": "poison", "save": {"save": "con_save", "difficulty": 13, "drops": "1d6"}}`: 3d6 on a failed save, a die less after each success, and over after three. A ruleset using it needs Capability API 1.635.

An applied condition's `checkEnds` may say `spendsItem: true`: made, the check also spends for good the item whose use put the condition on. And `failedRetryAfter: n`: failed, its holder's own tries are not offered again for `n` rounds. 5e's Iron Bands of Binding, `"checkEnds": {"check": "athletics", "difficulty": 20, "spendsItem": true, "failedRetryAfter": 14400}`, with a further use `{"kind": "buff", "budget": "bonus", "targetHolds": {"fromSelf": ["restrained"]}, "ends": {"conditions": ["restrained"]}}` for the command word that lets its prisoner go. A ruleset using either needs Capability API 1.636.

A `zone` may last up to 14400 rounds (`rounds`; 1000 before), as an Oil of Slipperiness's grease does for 8 hours (4800). It may say `close: {"budget": "action", "lingers": 100}`: its caster may close it on their turn, and it ends, or with `lingers` stays that many rounds more and spreads no more. And a burst may say `spreads: {"by": 10, "every": 10, "upTo": 120}`: it widens by `by` every `every` of its caster's turns, to a radius of `upTo` (distances in the ruleset's unit). 5e's Eversmoking Bottle, a 60-foot burst of heavy smoke at a point, `{"rounds": 14400, "anchor": "point", "triggers": [], "obscures": {...}, "close": {"budget": "action", "lingers": 100}, "spreads": {"by": 10, "every": 10, "upTo": 120}}`. A ruleset using any of these needs Capability API 1.637.

A combat condition may say `onFirstSeen: {"applies": {...}}`: as its holder first sees another creature, it ends and what `applies` names goes on them in its place, with that creature its source (the nearest, where they see several at once). 5e's Philter of Love: drunk, `{"condition": "love_struck", "effects": [], "onFirstSeen": {"applies": {"condition": "charmed", "duration": {"rounds": 600}}}}` for 100 rounds, so whoever the drinker sees first in the next 10 minutes charms them for an hour. A ruleset using it needs Capability API 1.638.

An item's `worn` or `carried` effect may add to its holder's proficiency bonus with `"proficiencyBonus": 1` (Capability API 1.639), from -6 to 6 and never 0, each item once. Where `resolution.proficiency.bonus` reads a derived value (5e's `proficiency_bonus`), it goes onto that value, so everything reading it has it too: skills, saves, attacks and a spell save DC. 5e's Ioun Stone of Mastery is `"worn": { "proficiencyBonus": 1 }`. A ruleset using it needs Capability API 1.639.

An item's use may cast other entries as Wish does (Capability API 1.640): a `"utility"` use with `"duplicates": { "catalog": "spells", "upTo": 8 }` (and `only` to narrow it) puts each entry of that catalog from the first level to `upTo` on the menu as `<item>: <use>: <entry>`, though its user knows none of them, each paid for with the item as the use is (its `charges`, or the item itself where it `consumes`), and done with the use's own `saveDifficulty` and `toHit` where it gives them. Such a use does nothing else. 5e's Ring of Three Wishes is `"use": { "kind": "utility", "budget": "action", "label": "Wish", "charges": 1, "duplicates": { "catalog": "spells", "upTo": 8 } }` with `charges.breaksOn` for its last charge. A ruleset using it needs Capability API 1.640.

Mounts that carry by weight (Capability API 1.641). `combat.mounts.weights` says what a rider weighs: `sheet` (a value read off their sheet, used where it gives more than 0, such as a derived value of body and gear) or else `bySize`, by the fight's own sizes. A creature whose block says `"mountCarries": { "weight": 400, "slowedUpTo": 800 }` may be mounted by riders of any size while what it bears, with them, is no more than `slowedUpTo` (or `weight` without it), and its speed is halved while it bears more than `weight`. Its first rider steers it as any mount; the rest are carried along wherever it goes, and when its rider gets down the next one on it takes it over. 5e's Carpet of Flying is a creature of its own, called by the carpet's item use (`calls`, `dismissible`), with its fly speed and `mountCarries` for its size: 3 by 5 feet `{ "weight": 200, "slowedUpTo": 400 }` at 80 feet, up to 6 by 9 feet `{ "weight": 800, "slowedUpTo": 1600 }` at 30. A ruleset using either needs Capability API 1.641.

A creature's `spellcasting` may say `slots`, its spell slots by level (Capability API 1.642): `{ "1": 4, "2": 3, "3": 2 }`. A spell of the trait with no `uses` and a level of 1 or more (its own `level`, else its entry's) spends one slot, the lowest it has left at or above that level, and is not offered once none is left; the menu shows the slots left at that level. With `coven`, the coven's spells spend slots shared by every member of the coven: 5e's hag covens. A spell may also give its own `saveDifficulty` and `toHit`, in place of the trait's, as a hag's innate spells are cast at a different DC from her coven's. A ruleset using either needs Capability API 1.642.

A creature's `sequence` step may roll for how many times it is made (Capability API 1.643): `"dice": { "count": 1, "sides": 4 }` in place of `times`, a violet fungus's 1d4 Rotting Touch attacks; the roll is logged and the sequence may be aimed at as many targets as the dice can come to; given fewer, the rolled strikes go round the targets given. `ifHit` names an earlier step by its place (counted from 0) whose attacks must all have hit the same creature: the step is then made at that creature and takes no target of its own, a grick's beak after its tentacles. `optional: true` lets the creature's maker leave the step out: the sequence is offered with a mode for each way of leaving optional steps out (at most 3, and never all of its steps), a gibbering mouther's Blinding Spittle. A ruleset using any of these needs Capability API 1.643.

A combat condition's `harms` may be `"at": "defies"` (Capability API 1.644): what the condition commands becomes a command its holder may break at a cost rather than something it cannot do. Its `forbids`, its `behavior` and its `cannot-harm` and `cannot-approach-source` effects stop nothing; the menu offers what they would have stopped, marked with the conditions it breaks (a walk marks only the cells it may not go to), and doing it deals the harm once the choice is done, only once while the condition holds with `once`. Such a condition commands something: one of those keys or effects. 5e's Geas, "takes 5d10 psychic damage each time it acts in a manner directly counter to your instructions, but no more than once each day": `{"at": "defies", "dice": "5d10", "type": "psychic", "once": true}` beside `forbids.casts` for "cast no spells", or `behavior.keepsIn` for "stay here". A ruleset using it needs Capability API 1.644.

A `wards`, a condition's or a zone's, may keep off only some entries of its catalogs (Capability API 1.645): `where`, the entries whose declared filter reads so as an amend's `where` reads it (5e's Mind Blank, `{"catalogs": ["spells"], "maxLevel": 9, "where": {"filter": "school", "equals": "divination"}}`), and `entries`, only these, each `<catalog>/<entry>` of a catalog the ward names (5e's Wish, "immunity to a single spell"). Both may be given; the ward then keeps off only what both allow. A ruleset using either needs Capability API 1.645.

Three pieces for 5e's Wish (Capability API 1.646). A combat condition's `harms` may be `"at": "casts"` with `catalogs`: its holder takes it each time they cast an entry of those catalogs, once for each level it was cast at with `perLevel`, and with `unblockable` past every resistance and immunity ("1d10 necrotic damage per level of that spell. This damage can't be reduced or prevented in any way"). An `applies` line may carry a `chance`, a d100 of that or lower for it to land, rolled for each one it would land on ("a 33 percent chance that you are unable to cast wish ever again"). And a condition's `forbids.entries`, each `<catalog>/<entry>`, stops those entries while it holds: put on `"until-dispelled"`, the lost Wish. A ruleset using any of these needs Capability API 1.646.

A combat condition with `"stasis": true` holds its holder out of time (Capability API 1.647), as 5e's Sequester does. Its holder takes no turns, so they make no death saves and nothing that runs on their own turns harms them or counts down. What others put on them that runs on those others' turns stops too: every other condition keeps the rounds it had, and no harm or save of any of them comes. The stasis condition itself still counts down on its source's turns where its duration runs on them, and ends as it says, with `"endsWhen": ["damaged"]` or a dispel. Give it `unseen` for the invisibility the spell gives. A ruleset using it needs Capability API 1.647.

A combat condition's `"languages"` says what its holder understands while it holds (Capability API 1.648), whatever their sheet or block says they speak. `"none"` is nothing at all, and nobody understands them either: 5e's Feeblemind, so a Command or Suggestion (`targetTypes.understands`) neither reaches them nor can be given by them. `"all"` is every language, and anybody who knows one understands them: Tongues. Where two conditions disagree, `"none"` wins. A ruleset using it needs Capability API 1.648.

A sense in `combat.senses` may say `"seesTrue": true` (Capability API 1.649): within its range and in sight, it sees things as they truly are. An image somebody called (`calls.illusion`) is never offered as a target to whoever sees it truly, and a condition's illusory `duplicates` draw none of their attacks. 5e's Witch Sight is such a sense, read off the sheet as 30 where the warlock has the invocation: `{ "id": "witch_sight", "label": "Witch Sight", "sheet": { "derived": "witch_sight" }, "seesTrue": true }`. A shapechanger's true form is for the Game Master to describe. A ruleset using it needs Capability API 1.649.

A condition's or an item's `fallsSafely` may be `{ "upTo": 20, "unless": ["incapacitated"] }` in place of `true` (Capability API 1.650): only a fall no higher than `upTo`, in the ruleset's distance, does its holder no harm and leaves them standing, and none does while they hold any of `unless`. 5e's Cat's Grace: "doesn't take damage from falling 20 feet or less if it isn't incapacitated". A ruleset using it needs Capability API 1.650.

Two keys for 5e's Dust of Sneezing and Choking (Capability API 1.651). A combat condition with `"suffocates": true` keeps its holder from breathing wherever they are: they hold their breath and then choke as `combat.underwater.breath` says, out of the water as in it, unless that breath's `exempt` spares them. And a condition's `"savesUnless": ["unconscious"]` keeps its save to end it from being rolled while its holder holds any of those conditions ("as long as it is conscious, a creature can repeat the saving throw"). A ruleset using either needs Capability API 1.651.

A save's `against` may name `qualities` (Capability API 1.652), what the thing saved against is: Cloudkill's and Stinking Cloud's `"against": { "qualities": ["gas"] }`. A save modifier's `against.qualities` then reaches it, as its `damage` reaches a save against that damage type: a Necklace of Adaptation's `{ "to": "saves", "mode": "advantage", "against": { "qualities": ["gas"] } }`. A ruleset using either needs Capability API 1.652.

A line of an item effect's or a sheet entry's `keepsOn` may be `{ "condition": id, "when": valueRef }` in place of a plain id (Capability API 1.653): that condition is kept on only while `when` reads above zero off its holder's sheet. A Holy Avenger keeps its 10-foot aura on below paladin level 17 and its 30-foot one from there, each a line whose `when` reads a derived value that is 1 at those levels; Aura of Protection does the same at level 18. A ruleset using it needs Capability API 1.653.

A combat condition's `weapon.die` now reaches a blow of flat damage too, when the condition names it by `names` or `items` (Capability API 1.654): the blow rolls one die of that size in place of its printed number, its ability's modifier still added. Alter Self's Natural Weapons, `{ "names": ["unarmed"], "die": 6 }`, make an unarmed strike's 1 plus Strength a d6 plus Strength. A ruleset relying on it should declare Capability API 1.654; an older Engine leaves the flat number as it was.

A combat condition's `whenSeen` now scopes its `attacks-against-disadvantage` too (Capability API 1.655): attacks against its holder are at disadvantage only from an attacker who can see them. A Robe of Scintillating Colors' shimmering light, "creatures that can see you have disadvantage on attack rolls against you", is a condition with that effect and `"whenSeen": true`. A ruleset using it needs Capability API 1.655.

Pools given back by count (Capability API 1.656). `recovers` may carry `count`, how many spent pools it gives back in all, at most; on a catalog entry the Game Master still names them, and a use naming more is refused. An item's `use` may carry `recovers` too, a "buff" with `targets: "self"` that must say its `count`: used, in a fight or out of one, it gives back the highest-level spent pools of the group itself, one at a time, each no higher than `most` and all within `upTo`. 5e's Pearl of Power is `{"group": "slots", "upTo": {"const": 3}, "most": 3, "count": 1}`, one spent slot of 3rd level or lower. A ruleset using it needs Capability API 1.656.

Ammunition with clauses of its own (Capability API 1.657). An item may carry `ammoRider`, `{"plus": [...], "spentOnUse"?: true}`: ammunition, drawn by a weapon's `ammo` by one of its tags, whose clauses (written as a weapon's `plus`, each with its own `targetIs`, `not`, `save` or `when: "critical"`) ride the blow it is shot with. Each carried stack of it is offered as its own way to shoot the weapon, named for the item, and a shot made the plain way takes plain ammunition first, then this. With `spentOnUse` it is spent once one of its clauses reaches a target, past a save that negates it: what of it would come back after a won fight comes back instead as its holder's plain ammunition of that tag, when they carry a stack of it. A weapon with a `clip` loads whatever comes first, so its shots carry no rider. 5e's Arrow of Dragon Slaying is `{"plus": [{"dice": "6d10", "type": "piercing", "save": {"save": "con_save", "difficulty": 17, "onSuccess": "half"}, "targetIs": {"types": ["dragon"]}}], "spentOnUse": true}` on an item tagged as arrows. A ruleset using it needs Capability API 1.657.

Attacks drawn to a holder (Capability API 1.658). A combat condition, or an item's `worn` effect, may carry `drawsAim`, `{"within", "blow"?, "weapon"?}`: an attack made at somebody else within `within` of its holder (in the ruleset's distance) is made at its holder instead. It is not a reaction: nothing is spent and nobody is asked, and the attack's own maker is never drawn. `blow` narrows it to blows struck in reach (`"melee"`) or from afar (`"ranged"`), and `weapon` to a weapon's blow. Where several draw it, the nearest to the target does; an area draws nothing, and neither does a fight without a board. 5e's Shield of Missile Attraction's curse is `{"within": 10, "blow": "ranged", "weapon": true}` on the shield's `worn`. A ruleset using it needs Capability API 1.658.

Zones that seal the air or stop shots (Capability API 1.659). A zone may carry `"sealsAir": true`: gases, wind and fog do not pass its edge, so an obscuring zone that is `dispersed`, or a wind (`disperses`, or a stage that does), covers only the cells on the side of it its own middle is on. A Fog Cloud outside stops at its face, one cast inside stays in, and a Gust of Wind outside never reaches fog inside. And a zone's `blocks` may name `"ranged"`, a wall or not: no attack from afar with a weapon, magical or not, is made into, out of or across it (a spell or an area is no weapon's). Either is what the zone does to its ground, so it needs no trigger. 5e's Cube of Force is a burst around its caster: its first face `"sealsAir": true`, its second `"blocks": ["ranged"]`. A ruleset using it needs Capability API 1.659.

A mount's speed past its full load (Capability API 1.660). A creature's `mountCarries` may carry `slowedSpeed`, in the ruleset's distance, beside `slowedUpTo`: bearing more than `weight`, it moves at no more than that instead of at half speed. 5e's Broom of Flying is `{"weight": 200, "slowedUpTo": 400, "slowedSpeed": 30}` on a 50-foot flier. A ruleset using it needs Capability API 1.660.

Starting gear options for some sheets (Capability API 1.661). An option of a starting gear choice may carry `offeredWhen`, a value read off the sheet as a catalog entry's is: it is offered only while that reads above zero, and taking it otherwise is refused. A choice none of whose options is offered to the sheet asks nothing, and where there is no sheet to read every option is offered. 5e's cleric's "(a) a mace or (b) a warhammer (if proficient)" gives the warhammer's option an `offeredWhen` reading a derived value that is 1 with martial weapon proficiency. A ruleset using it needs Capability API 1.661.

Conditions that change derived values (Capability API 1.662). A combat condition may carry `derivedChanges`, by derived id: `{"times"?, "add"?}`, at most 12. While its holder holds it, on the sheet or in a fight, the derived value is multiplied by every `times` as it is worked out, then every `add` goes on top, and everything worked out after it reads the changed value. 5e's Enhance Ability (Bull's Strength) is `{"carry_capacity": {"times": 2}}`. A ruleset using it needs Capability API 1.662.

Conditions that last a number of days (Capability API 1.663). An applied condition with `duration: "until-dispelled"` may carry `lastsDays`, a value read off the sheet of whoever puts it on as it goes on. In a fight it has no clock. When the fight ends, a sheet that still holds it keeps how many days are left, and the sheet's `pass` command counts them down, taking the condition off once they are gone. Taking it off another way drops the days with it. Quivering Palm is `{"condition": "quivering", "duration": "until-dispelled", "lastsDays": {"field": "level"}}`. `lastsDays` with any other duration is refused. A ruleset using it needs Capability API 1.663.

Conditions that last while an item is worn (Capability API 1.664). A condition an item's `use` applies may carry `whileItemWorn: true`, for an item that takes a slot or binds. The sheet remembers which inventory stack keeps it on. When that item is taken off, unbound or no longer carried, the condition comes off the sheet with that inventory change, and a fight that starts with the item off does not bring it in. Used in a fight, it remembers the stack there too, and the sheet keeps that once the fight is over. Armor of Invulnerability is `{"condition": "invulnerable", "duration": {"rounds": 100}, "whileItemWorn": true}`. A catalog entry's own `applies` may not use it. A ruleset using it needs Capability API 1.664.

Called creatures that may ignore every order (Capability API 1.665). A call's `disobeys` is `{"chance": 1-99, "when"?}`, `when` being sheet bounds (`value`, `atLeast`, `atMost`) read off the caller. As they are called, while `when` holds, a d100 at or under `chance` leaves them under nobody's control, as `side: "none"` does, and a dismissal does not send them away. The log says they ignore every order. The obsidian steed is `"disobeys": {"chance": 10, "when": {"value": {"field": "good"}, "atLeast": 1}}` on a sheet that keeps alignment in a `good` field. A ruleset using it needs Capability API 1.665.

Kinds left out of a ward, and an inside set by a tag (Capability API 1.666). A zone set up before a fight (1.462) may say `"spares": [<kind>, ...]`, creature types by id or name that its `keepsOut` no longer keeps out. Each must be one it keeps out, or it is left as it was and the fight says so. It may also say `"tag": <tag>`, so what it does inside works only on creatures carrying that tag. Hallow set against fiends and undead but sparing fiends is `{"entry": "Hallow", "spares": ["fiend"]}`, and its extra effect on a deity's followers is `{"entry": "Hallow", "tag": "follower"}` with that tag on those creatures. Nothing in a ruleset changes for these, but a ruleset relying on them needs Capability API 1.666.

What an item asks of its bearer or user (Capability API 1.667). An item's `binds.needs` lists up to 4 sheet bounds (`value`, `atLeast`, `atMost`) its bearer must meet. Bound by somebody who does not meet them, it reads as not worn: its worn effect and stats do not count and its uses are not offered, in a fight or out of one. An item's `userNeeds` lists the same for using it at all: out of a fight the use is refused, and in a fight it is not offered. `items.needsWaivedBy`, a value off the sheet, waives both while it is above zero. 5e's "requires attunement by a cleric" is `"binds": {"needs": [{"value": {"field": "cleric_level"}, "atLeast": 1}]}`, a cleric's spell scroll is `"userNeeds": [{"value": {"field": "cleric_level"}, "atLeast": 1}]`, and Use Magic Device is `"needsWaivedBy": {"derived": "use_magic_device"}` with that derived value 1 from rogue level 13. A ruleset using any of them needs Capability API 1.667.

A contest that takes what its target holds (Capability API 1.668). A catalog entry's `contest` (1.565) may say `takes`, with `slots` and/or `tags`. On a target it wins against, the first thing that target holds in one of those slots, or with one of those tags, lies at its user's feet: the target can pick it up again only from there, as with anything dropped (1.266). The log says what was taken. 5e's Telekinesis on an object a creature holds is `"contest": {"attacker": ["spellcasting"], "defender": ["strength"], "takes": {"slots": ["hand"]}}`. A ruleset using it needs Capability API 1.668.

Traps disarmed with a check (Capability API 1.669). A zone's `disarm` makes it a trap its caster's foes may take apart: `{"check": "thieves_tools", "difficulty": 15, "tripsOnFailBy": 5}`. Whoever sees it (a hidden one only once their side has found it) and stands within a cell of it is offered "Disarm <its label>" through the `disarm` standard action, which `combat.standard` must list. They throw `check` (one of `combat.checks`) against `difficulty`, or the entry's own save difficulty when it is left out. Made, the trap is gone without going off. Failed by `tripsOnFailBy` or more, it goes off, at whoever tried where entering it is what sets it off (`detonates.entered`). A `standard` grant naming `disarm` lets another budget pay for it, as 5e's Fast Hands does with the bonus action. A ruleset using it needs Capability API 1.669.

A teleport's familiarity table (Capability API 1.670). A `teleport` that `leaves` may carry `familiarity`, one to eight rows of how well its user knows where it goes, each an `id`, a `label` and its chances in a hundred of a `mishap`, of a `similar` area and of landing `off` target (the rest lands on target), with `mishap`, what a mishap deals each traveller: `{"dice": "3d10", "type": "force"}`. Each row is offered as a mode, `familiar-<id>`, and one must be picked to leave the fight. As they leave, a d100 is rolled against the row picked, mishap first, then similar, then off target. A mishap hurts its user and everybody it carries, and rolls again (twenty times at most). Whoever is still alive, standing or down, then leaves. The log says each roll and what came of it. 5e's Teleport writes its rows as the spell's table: a permanent circle at 0, very familiar at 5, 8 and 11, seen casually at 33, 10 and 10, and a false destination at 50 and 50. A ruleset using it needs Capability API 1.670.

A climb kept off some ground (Capability API 1.671). A combat condition with a climb in its `speeds` may say `climbNotOn`: `terrain`, tiles (or a board's place) where that climb is no good, and `slippery: true`, ground a lasting area makes slippery, which a zone's `terrain` marks with `"slippery": true` (5e's Grease). On a board whose place `terrain` names, the condition gives no climb at all. A climb of its holder's own, or one another condition gives without `climbNotOn`, still climbs anywhere. 5e's Slippers of Spider Climbing keep on a condition with `"speeds": {"climb": "walk"}` and `"climbNotOn": {"slippery": true, "terrain": ["ice"]}`. A ruleset using either needs Capability API 1.671.

Fields held to a combined most (Capability API 1.672). The sheet may say `totals`, up to eight of `{"fields": [...], "max": N, "label"?: "..."}`, each two to twenty-four number fields whose values together may come to no more than `max`. The full sheet form lowers each field's most by what the others hold (never below its own least), the Game Master's advancement refuses a value past it, and a sheet already over it, an imported one or one from before, says so above its fields without changing them. The guided builder's step `total` (1.107) still holds its own step. 5e's class levels: `{"fields": ["level_barbarian", "level_bard", ...], "max": 20, "label": "Class levels"}`. A ruleset using it needs Capability API 1.672.

Creature saves failed badly (Capability API 1.673). A creature action's `save` may carry `failedBy` as a catalog entry's does (1.592): `{"atLeast": 5, "applies": [...]}` puts those conditions on in place of the action's `applies` when the target fails by that much or more, as 5e's mummy's Dreadful Glare, frightened and paralyzed on a failure by 5 or more. On a creature or a catalog entry, `failedBy` may say `resultAtMost` instead of `atLeast`: a failed save whose total is that or lower, as a sprite's arrow, poisoned and unconscious when the save's result is 5 or lower. A ruleset using either needs Capability API 1.673.

Creature actions used once on each creature (Capability API 1.674). A creature action may say `oncePerTarget`: `{"whenSaved": true}` spares a creature that made its save, `{"whenEnds": true}` one whose condition from it has ended, both together either, and neither spares everybody it was used on. A spared creature is never picked or caught by it again in the fight, and with `"until": "<rest id>"` a character remembers it on their sheet until they take that rest. `"shared": "<id>"` makes every creature whose action names the same id one source; otherwise each creature (by name) and action is its own. 5e's dragons' Frightful Presence: `{"whenSaved": true, "whenEnds": true, "until": "long"}`; a mummy's Dreadful Glare adds `"shared": "mummy-glare"`, the mummy lord's its own. A ruleset using it needs Capability API 1.674.

Pulling toward whoever pulls (Capability API 1.675). A creature action or a catalog entry may say `pull`, a distance in the ruleset's own unit: whoever it lands on (on a failed save, when it has one) is pulled straight toward its user, a cell at a time from their nearest cells, and stops once beside them, at a wall, a filled cell or a steep rise. A fall off a step works as it does for a push. An action pushes or pulls, not both. 5e's balor whip `"pull": 25` with its DC 20 Strength save; a roper's Reel `"pull": 25` with `"targetHolds": {"fromSelf": ["grappled"]}`; Thorn Whip and Lightning Lure `"pull": 10`. The log says "pulls ... in". A ruleset using it needs Capability API 1.675.

Creatures that hang on (Capability API 1.676). A combat condition may say `attaches`: whoever put it on hangs on to its holder, carried along into the last cell they passed as they walk or are pushed, and draws no opportunity attacks. `"sourceAttacks": "none"` lets it attack nobody while it hangs on (a stirge); `"holder"` nobody but its holder (a cloaker, a darkmantle). `endsAfterHarm` lets go once the condition's `harms` have dealt that much in all (a stirge's 10, with `harms` `{"at": "source-turn-start", "dice": "1d4", "flat": 3}`). Walking away ends it with the applies' `endsAway`, anybody's action with `checkEnds` `"by": "any"`, and its source falling with `endsWhenSourceDown`; a cloaker's Damage Transfer is `takesSourceHarm` `{"share": 0.5}`. An applies entry may say `"withAdvantage": true` to go on only from an attack rolled with advantage: the cloaker's bite on the head, `blinded` with `endsWith` its hold. A ruleset using either needs Capability API 1.676.

What a creature's blow does to whoever it drops (Capability API 1.677). A creature action may say `onFell`: when its blow takes somebody who was not already down to nothing, `"stabilizes": true` leaves them stable (it needs a dying rule) and `applies` puts its conditions on them, with the creature as their source. A blow that leaves them standing, or lands on somebody already down, does none of it. 5e's giant spider bite: `{"stabilizes": true, "applies": [{"condition": "poisoned", "duration": {"rounds": 600}}, {"condition": "paralyzed", "duration": "instant", "endsWith": "poisoned"}]}`; healing never ends a condition unless the ruleset says so, so "even after regaining hit points" needs nothing more. A ruleset using it needs Capability API 1.677.

A creature's slaying held to the low, and its failed save dropping to no health (Capability API 1.678). Beside `slaysOnFailedSave`, a creature action may say `slaysHealthAtMost`: only a target with that much health or less as it fails is slain, and any other takes the blow as if it had saved (a solar's Slaying Longbow, 190). A creature action may say `dropsOnFailedSave` as a catalog entry may: a target that fails drops to no health, dying where the ruleset has dying, and one that saves is untouched (a sea hag's Death Glare, with `"onSuccess": "negates"`). It goes with the action's own save and not with `slaysOnFailedSave`. A ruleset using either on a creature needs Capability API 1.678.

Flyby (Capability API 1.679). A combat condition may say `"flyby": true`: its holder, while it has a flying speed, leaves anybody's reach without drawing an opportunity attack, since it flies out of it. Kept on a creature with `keepsOn`, it is 5e's Flyby on the owl, giant owl and flying snake; without a flying speed it does nothing. A ruleset using it needs Capability API 1.679.

Sharing spaces (Capability API 1.680). A combat condition may say `"sharesSpace": true`: its holder walks into anybody's space and may stop there, friend or foe, and anybody may walk into and stop in its own. Kept on a creature with `keepsOn`, it is 5e's swarms, which "can occupy another creature's space and vice versa". Others still keep each other out as before. A ruleset using it needs Capability API 1.680.

Squeezing, and moving as a smaller size (Capability API 1.681). `combat.sizes.squeezing` `{"costs": 2, "condition": "squeezed"}` lets somebody whose whole space does not fit between walls move through as the size below their own: each such step costs `costs` times over, and where they stop squeezed they fill only that smaller space and hold `condition` (give it the effects the ruleset wants: 5e's disadvantage on attacks and Dexterity saves and advantage to attacks at them). Once they stop where they fit whole, they fill all of it again and the condition goes. A combat condition may say `"squeezesAs": "<size id>"`: its holder moves through any opening that fits that size freely, at a plain step's cost and with no condition: 5e's Amorphous and swarms, `"tiny"`. A ruleset using either needs Capability API 1.681.

A condition held only while an ally is seen near (Capability API 1.682). A combat condition's `heldOnlyIn` may say `allySeenWithin`, a distance in the ruleset's own unit, beside or instead of `light`: the condition holds only while its holder sees an ally of theirs standing within it, and is held off, not ended, otherwise. Off a board any ally standing will do. 5e's bearded devil's Steadfast is a kept-on condition with `"conditionImmunities": ["frightened"]` and `"heldOnlyIn": {"allySeenWithin": 30}`. A ruleset using it needs Capability API 1.682.

A poison on a weapon (Capability API 1.683): a condition's `onHit` can deal damage of its own, `"damage": {"dice": "1d4", "flat": 0, "type": "poison"}`, and ask a save first, `"save": {"save": "con_save", "difficulty": 11, "onSuccess": "half"}`; made, the damage is halved (or with `"negates"`, not dealt) and its `applies` do not go on, so `applies` is now optional when `damage` is given. `"failedBy": {"atLeast": 5, "applies": [...]}` puts more on when the save fails by that much or more (drow poison's unconsciousness), and needs the `save`. `"damageTypes": ["slashing", "piercing"]` limits it to hits of those damage types, so a poison coating a blade never rides a club. Keep the coating on its holder with `endsAfter: "own-hit"` for a one-hit dose, or a duration for a longer one.

A condition that ends when its holder is healed (Capability API 1.684): `endsWhen` takes `healed`, ended by any heal in a fight that gives its holder back at least one point (caltrops' slowed walk, "until the creature regains at least 1 hit point"), and `healed-by-magic`, ended only by such a heal from something magical: an action, entry or item use whose qualities say `"magical"`, as `cannot-heal-nonmagical` reads it (an Infernal Wound, "closes if the target receives magical healing"). A heal that gives nothing back, at full health or kept off by a condition, ends neither. `endsWhen` now holds up to all six endings.

A creature's action that sends somebody, or itself, out of the fight (Capability API 1.685): a stat block's or catalog creature's action takes `dismisses` as a catalog entry does, `true` or `{"while": conditionId, "gone"?, "turns"?}`, the `while` condition one of the action's own `applies`. With `"self": true` and `"turns": true` the creature itself leaves, still taking its turns away, where it is offered only the way back, the condition's `endable` "End", and returns where it left or the nearest free place. A ghost's Etherealness: `{"id": "etherealness", "name": "Etherealness", "budget": "action", "self": true, "applies": [{"condition": "ethereal", "duration": "until-dispelled"}], "dismisses": {"while": "ethereal", "turns": true}}` with the combat condition `{"condition": "ethereal", "effects": [], "endable": {"budget": "action"}}`; a phase spider's Ethereal Jaunt is the same on a bonus action. `"carries": {"count": 3, "within": 5, "condition": "ethereal"}` (on a `self` action) takes up to that many of its standing allies within that distance, nearest first, each away while it holds `condition` from the creature, and brings them back when the creature returns (a nightmare's Ethereal Stride); a rider whose condition is `endable` may also come back on its own.

Up to 16 combat checks (Capability API 1.686): `combat.checks` holds 16, 12 before, so a ruleset with all of 5e's contest skills still has room for a thieves' tools check that a disarm reads. A ruleset with more than 12 needs Capability API 1.686.

A creature's action whose uses each do something else (Capability API 1.687): `"byUse": [actionId, ...]`, two to six other plain actions of the same creature, on an action with `uses` and nothing of its own to do (no attack roll, damage, heal, save, applies, sequence or `self`). Its first use makes the first action named, at whom it was aimed, its second the second, and any use past the end the last; the uses are counted by its own `uses`, so a creature's day of them carries the count. The actions named are never on the menu themselves. An androsphinx's Roar, "each time it roars before finishing a long rest, the roar is louder": `{"id": "roar", "name": "Roar", "budget": "action", "uses": {"per": "day", "count": 3}, "byUse": ["roar_1", "roar_2", "roar_3"]}`, each roar its own action with its own area, save and conditions.

A Periapt of Wound Closure (Capability API 1.688). A combat condition's `"stabilizesWhenDying": true` makes its holder, dying as a turn of theirs starts, stable in place of the dying roll, the log saying so; kept on by a worn item's `keepsOn`, it is the periapt's "you stabilize whenever you are dying at the start of your turn". A rest's `dice` entry may say `"times"`, a value reference that multiplies what each die heals, its `add` included, never less than once: `{"itemStat": {"stat": "hit_die_times", "from": "worn", "pick": "max", "default": 1}}` with the periapt giving `hit_die_times` 2 doubles "the number of hit points it restores" while it is worn.

Ground a careful walker crosses safely, and ground that stops a walk (Capability API 1.689). A lasting area's `terrain` may say `"spareSlowWalkers": true`: while one stands, a walker is offered "Move carefully" beside "Move", on which every step into such ground costs double (half speed there), and the area's `enter` trigger lets them by. And `"endsWalk": true`: caught by its `enter` trigger and failing its save (or with no save to make), the walker stops in that cell with no movement left this turn. 5e's caltrops, `"triggers": ["enter"]` with `"terrain": {"spareSlowWalkers": true, "endsWalk": true}`, a DC 15 Dexterity save, 1 piercing damage and the slowed walk that ends when healed (1.684); ball bearings, `spareSlowWalkers` with the prone save.

A number read from the words of a text field (Capability API 1.690). A derived value `{"op": "wordsTable", "from": {"field": "alignment"}, "table": {"good": 1, "evil": -1}, "default": 0}` reads the field as a player wrote it: each key is a word or phrase (1 to 40 of them, each up to 40 characters) matched as whole words ignoring case, the first key in the table's order the field names gives the number, and a field naming none reads `default`. So "Lawful Good" reads 1 and "Goodly" reads 0. The field is text (or an enum). It reads like any derived value, so a condition, check or creature rule keyed on alignment reads it as a number: 5e's Book of Exalted Deeds or a Talisman of Pure Good harming the evil who touch it.

An item asking any one of what it names (Capability API 1.691). Beside `binds.needs`, `"needsAny": true` makes the binding met when any one of the needs is, not all of them: 5e's "requires attunement by a cleric, druid, or warlock", one need per class level, each `atLeast: 1`. `"userNeedsAny": true` does the same for `userNeeds`: a spell scroll on the bard, cleric and wizard lists, read by any of the three. Either may name up to 8 needs (4 before 1.691). `items.needsWaivedBy` still waives them all.

A contest's `takes` as a mode of its own (Capability API 1.692). Beside `contest.takes`, `"takesMode": {"label": "Object", "restLabel": "Creature"}` offers the entry in two modes, picked as it is used like a weapon's mode. In `label`'s mode a win takes what `takes` names and does nothing else (no `applies`, `movesTarget` or damage); in `restLabel`'s mode, and when no mode is picked, a win does all the rest and takes nothing. It goes without `appliesOneOf`, `cureOneOf`, `kindChoice`, `forms`, `calls` and `conjures`. A repeat (`repeats`) of any entry cast in a mode is now offered and made only in the mode the cast was made in (the first mode when none was picked): Telekinesis keeps its grip on the object or the creature it held, and Hex keeps its ability.

A use whose user saves against what it puts on them (Capability API 1.693). Beside `appliesSelf` and `save`, `"appliesSelfSaves": true` has the user roll that save too, at the same difficulty, and `appliesSelf` goes on them only if they fail. 5e's Dust of Sneezing and Choking, whose thrower stands in the cloud with everybody else: the item's use with its Constitution save, the choking condition in both `applies` and `appliesSelf`.

A lasting area anybody near may move on a check (Capability API 1.694). A zone's `move` may carry `"check": {"check": "arcana", "difficulty": 25, "within": 60, "distanceBy": {"abilityMod": "int", "times": 5, "atLeast": 5}, "failsToward": 10}`. Then nobody moves it by `move` alone: anybody standing within `within` of its middle, its caster too, may spend `move.budget` on "Control" it, throwing `check` (one of `combat.checks`) against `difficulty`. Met, it goes to a cell they pick, as far as `distanceBy.times` × their `abilityMod` modifier (at least `atLeast`; `move.distance` without `distanceBy`), and it is theirs. Failed, it comes `failsToward` at them. While somebody else moved it last and still stands, the check is contested by theirs instead, the tie theirs, and a loss moves nothing. Moved onto somebody, it catches them as entering it does. 5e's Sphere of Annihilation, its 4d10 force on a failed DC 13 Dexterity save as the zone's `enter`, with `outlasts` so it stays when whoever made it is gone.

A creature's action whose riders wait on a contest (Capability API 1.695). A creature's action may carry `"contest": {"attacker": ["might"], "defender": ["might"]}` (ids of `combat.checks`, `ties` the defender's unless it says `"attacker"`): on a target it lands on, its damage lands either way, and what it puts on, pushes or pulls goes on only if the creature's best check beats the target's, as a save that does nothing on a success would. Never beside a `save`. 5e's merrow Harpoon, "a Strength contest against the merrow or be pulled up to 20 feet toward the merrow", with `pull: 20`.

A creature that goes into whom it lands on (Capability API 1.696). A creature action's `dismisses` may say `"possesses": true` beside `while`: on a target that holds `while` from it after the action lands, the creature itself leaves the fight, not the target, and comes back beside them as soon as they no longer hold it from the creature, however it ended. Give `while` a combat condition with `"controls": true` (1.577), so the target fights on the creature's side while it holds, even with the creature away, and `"endsWhenHolder": {"downed": true}`, so it ends as they drop. A ghost's Possession: `{"id": "possession", "budget": "action", "reach": 5, "save": {"save": "cha_save", "difficulty": 13, "onSuccess": "negates"}, "applies": [{"condition": "possessed", "duration": "until-dispelled"}], "dismisses": {"while": "possessed", "possesses": true}}`; Dispel Evil and Good or Break Enchantment ending `possessed` brings the ghost out too. Never with `turns`, `gone` or `carries`, nor on a `self` action. Give the condition `"endable": {"budget": "bonus"}` (1.622) for the ghost to leave at will as a bonus action, taken on the body's turn. Dominate Person, Beast and Monster need nothing new: their condition with `"controls": true` puts the dominated on the caster's side while it holds.

More conditions kept from whoever a zone holds (Capability API 1.697). A zone's `keepsOut.cantGive` and `keepsOut.frees` may each name up to 12 conditions (8 before 1.697): Magic Circle and Hallow's "can't be charmed, frightened, or possessed" beside the rest a ruleset folds into those words.

A creature's action whose riders go only on some targets (Capability API 1.698). A creature's action may carry `"ridersIs"`, a target test like any other (`types`, `tags`, `upToSize`, `atLeastSize`, ...): what it puts on (`applies`), pushes (`push`), pulls (`pull`) or sends away (`dismisses`) goes only on a target that is it, and its `contest` (1.695) is thrown only with one; its damage lands on anybody it hits. 5e's merrow Harpoon, "If the target is Huge or smaller, it must succeed on a Strength contest against the merrow or be pulled up to 20 feet toward the merrow": `"contest": {"attacker": ["athletics"], "defender": ["athletics"]}, "pull": 20, "ridersIs": {"upToSize": "huge"}`. The same shape suits a grapple or a swallow that holds only a creature of some size. The action needs something for it to narrow.

Tags read off an alignment in words (Capability API 1.699). A creature may say `"alignment": "lawful evil"`, and `combat.creatureTypes` may say `"tagsFrom": "alignment"`, a character's text or choice field. Each of `creatureTypes.tags` the words name by label or id, as whole words ignoring case, is one of its tags, beside any it names in `tags`; a word after "non-" or "not" names nothing, so "any non-good alignment" is no `good`. Declare tags such as `lawful`, `chaotic`, `good`, `evil` and `neutral`, and every target test reads alignment as it reads any tag: a Talisman of Pure Good's fissure that slays only the evil, `"targetTypes": {"is": {"tags": ["evil"]}}`; a Holy Avenger's or Protection from Evil and Good's narrowing the same way. "Any alignment" and "unaligned" name nothing.

An item's stats for some wearers only (Capability API 1.700). An item may carry `statsIf`, up to 4 lines of `{"needs": [...], "needsAny": true, "add": {"spell_attack_bonus": 2}}`: while it is worn (and bound where it binds), each line whose `needs` its wearer's sheet meets, as `binds.needs` (1.667) reads them, all of them or any one with `needsAny`, adds `add` to the item's own number `stats` (a missing one counting 0), so every `itemStat` reading them counts it. 5e's Talisman of Pure Good: attuned only by the good (`binds.needs` on the alignment's `wordsTable`, 1.690), and `"statsIf": [{"needs": [{"value": {"track": "cleric_level"}, "atLeast": 1}, {"value": {"track": "paladin_level"}, "atLeast": 1}], "needsAny": true, "add": {"spell_attack_bonus": 2}}]` for its "+2 to spell attack rolls" for a good cleric or paladin.

An item that harms whoever takes it up (Capability API 1.701). An item may carry `touchHarms`, up to 4 lines of `{"needs": [...], "needsAny": true, "dice": "8d6", "flat": 0, "type": "radiant"}`: whenever it comes into somebody's hands in the game's inventory (added to their bag, given to them, or more of it gained), the first line whose `needs` their sheet meets, as `binds.needs` (1.667) reads them, and a line with no `needs` meets anybody, is rolled and taken off their health (one mark of the lightest kind on a wound track), and the journal says so. 5e's Talisman of Pure Good, with an alignment `wordsTable` (1.690) reading good 1, evil -1: `[{"needs": [{"value": {"derived": "goodness"}, "atMost": -1}], "dice": "8d6", "type": "radiant"}, {"needs": [{"value": {"derived": "goodness"}, "atMost": 0}], "dice": "6d6", "type": "radiant"}]`. A fake hurts as what it truly is (1.79). The ruleset says what health is.

An area of only its own cell (Capability API 1.702). A burst's `area`, a catalog entry's or an item use's, may say `"oneCell": true`: it covers only the cell it is set down at, however small its `size`, where a burst otherwise covers at least the cells around it. Moved (`zone.move`, 1.173; on a check, 1.694) or drifting, it stays one cell. 5e's Sphere of Annihilation, a 2-foot sphere: `"area": {"shape": "burst", "size": 2, "oneCell": true}` with its `zone`. Only a single burst held at a point: not `around`, `copies` or `sizePerCostStep`.

A zone moved on a check that a talisman helps with (Capability API 1.703). A zone's `move.check` (1.694) may carry `"holding": {"tags": ["sphere_talisman"], "adds": {"derived": "proficiency_bonus"}, "advantage": true, "distanceBy": {"abilityMod": "int", "times": 10, "add": 10}}`. A mover holding an item with one of `tags` (worn on their sheet and not dropped, or a thing a creature holds) adds `adds`, read off their sheet, to the check, throws it with `advantage`, and, while they already control the zone (they moved it last), may move it as far as `distanceBy`: `add` plus `times` × their ability modifier, at least `atLeast`. Each part is optional, one at least. 5e's Talisman of the Sphere, "you double your proficiency bonus on the check ... levitate it 10 feet plus a number of additional feet equal to 10 × your Intelligence modifier": `adds` the proficiency bonus and that `distanceBy`.

Actions that never hinder a creature (Capability API 1.704). A condition's `"unhinderedBy": ["Web"]` names actions, by id or label, that never hinder its holder: a lasting area one of them leaves does not catch them, so its ground is no hard going for them and it strikes and puts nothing on them, and what one of them puts on them (a hit's restraint) does not go on, logged as immune. 5e's Web Walker, kept on a giant spider with `keepsOn`: "ignores movement restrictions caused by webbing", the Web spell's and its own Web attack's.

Attacks that ignore some cover (Capability API 1.705). A condition's `"coverIgnored": {"upTo": "half", "attacks": "spell"}` lets its holder's attacks ignore cover up to `upTo` (`"half"` or `"three-quarters"`), the half a cell's own ground gives included. `attacks` (`"weapon"`, `"spell"` or `"any"`) and `blow` (`"melee"` or `"ranged"`) narrow it to some attacks. Cover past `upTo` counts in full, and total cover always does. 5e's Wand of the War Mage, kept on by the wand with `keepsOn`: "you ignore half cover when making a spell attack". Sharpshooter is `{"upTo": "three-quarters", "attacks": "weapon", "blow": "ranged"}`, Spell Sniper `{"upTo": "three-quarters", "attacks": "spell"}`.

An item that hovers (Capability API 1.706). An item's `toggle` may carry `"freesSlots": true` and `"rounds": 10`. While it is on, the slots it takes are free, so a versatile weapon beside it deals its `versatile` dice and the inventory has room for something else in its hand, and it still does all it does worn. It goes off by itself after `rounds` of its holder's turns, and as the fight ends; turned off in a fight with no room for it, it falls (logged as dropped). An item with `freesSlots` takes some `slots`. 5e's Animated Shield: `{"label": "Animate", "budget": "bonus", "freesSlots": true, "rounds": 10}`, "it hovers in your space to protect you as if you were wielding it, leaving your hands free".

A creature's call commanded, striking as its caller (Capability API 1.707). A creature action's `calls` may carry `"commandedWith": "bonus"`, as a catalog entry's (1.558): those it calls act only as their caller commands, their caller paying 1 of that budget at each of their turns but the first. And `"fromCaller": {"action": "greatsword"}`: their attacks are made with that one of their caller's own attacks' bonus to hit and damage. 5e's solar, Flying Sword: `{"creature": "creatures/flying-sword", "count": {"flat": 1}, "commandedWith": "bonus", "fromCaller": {"action": "greatsword"}, "endsWithCaller": true}`.

Ground some creatures cross easily, or are harmed in (Capability API 1.708). A condition's `"easyGround": ["snow", "Sleet Storm"]` makes hard going cost its holder a plain step on a tile it names, anywhere on a board whose place it names, and in a lasting area left by an action it names by id or label. 5e's Ice Walk on a white dragon, kept on with `keepsOn`: "difficult terrain composed of ice or snow doesn't cost it extra movement", a snowy board's and Sleet Storm's ice. Its `"harmedEntering": {"terrain": ["water"], "zones": ["Wall of Water"], "harm": {"flat": 1, "damageType": "cold"}}` deals its holder that harm, with no save, for each cell they walk into on one of those tiles or in one of those actions' lasting areas; one of `terrain` and `zones` at least. 5e's fire elemental's Water Susceptibility, "for every 5 feet the elemental moves in water ... it takes 1 cold damage".

Massive damage (Capability API 1.709). The combat block's `dying` may carry `"instantDeath": "overflow-at-least-max"`: when a blow takes one who would lie dying to 0 and the damage left over is at least their hit point maximum, they die outright instead, and a blow at least that big while they are down kills them too. Temporary hit points soak first, and a form with health of its own counts only what carries past it. A creature that dies at 0 anyway is not affected. Hanging on at 1 holds against it only where it wards off being slain outright (`hangsOn.outright`, 1.596): Death Ward holds, Relentless Endurance does not. The log says they were killed by massive damage. On Held Ground they are felled as always.

The bonus-action spell rule (Capability API 1.710). The combat block may carry `"casting": {"afterBonusSpell": {"catalogs": ["spells"], "budget": "bonus", "only": {"level": 0, "budget": "action"}}}`. On their own turn, once someone has cast an entry of those catalogs with the bonus budget, the only entry of them they are offered for the rest of that turn is one of at most `only.level` cast with `only.budget`: a cantrip with an action. And once they have cast one any other way, a leveled spell with an action say, they are offered none with the bonus budget that turn. A cantrip cast with an action first leaves a bonus-action spell open. A level is what the entry is paid at, 0 for a cantrip. Casting from an item whose use casts from those catalogs, a scroll, counts. A Quickened spell counts as cast with the bonus budget. Casting on someone else's turn, a reaction or a readied spell let go, is not counted.

What a target wears (Capability API 1.711). Any target test (`targetIs`, `is`, `not`, `except`, `anyOf`) may carry `"wears": {"tags": ["metal"]}`: only one wearing an item tagged one of these passes, an item worn on their sheet, or for a creature what its own `"wears": ["armor", "metal"]` says. 5e's Shocking Grasp, advantage against a target in metal armor, Heat Metal's target, and a rust monster's Antennae. Wearing alone is a whole test, and beside types or tags both must hold.

A save on damage that leans (Capability API 1.712). A condition's `saveWhenDamaged` (1.125), which repeats its `saveEnds` save each time its holder takes damage, may be `{"lean": "advantage"}` in place of `true`: that save is rolled with advantage (or disadvantage). The save at the moment `saveEnds.at` names is rolled as before. 5e's Hideous Laughter: `saveEnds` at turn end, and `"saveWhenDamaged": {"lean": "advantage"}` on its condition. Where a `seenLean` (1.268) also leans the save, opposite leans cancel.

A character's tags off several fields (Capability API 1.713). `combat.creatureTypes.tagsFrom` (1.699) may be a list of text or choice fields, `["race", "alignment"]`: each is read as one is, every `tags` entry named in any of them by label or id as whole words, and the tags of all of them kept. A sheet whose race reads "High Elf" carries `elf`, so a ghoul's Claws with `ridersIs {"except": {"tags": ["elf"]}}` (1.698) passes over an elf player character as it does an elf creature.

A condition held only on some ground (Capability API 1.714). A condition's `heldOnlyIn` (1.605) may name `"ground": ["water", "Running Water"]`: it holds only while its holder stands where one of these names is a tile of the board, the board's whole place (its environment), or a lasting area left by an action named by its id or label, any cell they fill counting; elsewhere, and off a board, it is held off, not ended. 5e's vampire: a kept-on condition with `"heldOnlyIn": {"ground": [...]}` and an aura that `harms` itself (`self: true`) for 20 acid. A board has one water tile, so naming `water` counts all of a board's water as running; a ruleset that keeps still water apart names only a lasting area of running water instead, left by an action such as a river's current or Control Water. Beside `light` or `allySeenWithin`, every one of them must hold.

Not while holding somebody (Capability API 1.715). A creature's action, or a catalog entry's mechanics, may carry `"unlessImposing": ["grappled"]`: it is offered only while its user has put none of these conditions on anybody else still holding it. 5e's ankheg, whose Acid Spray is used only while it is not grappling.

A creature's own jump (Capability API 1.716). A creature may carry `"jump": {"long": 30, "high": 15, "withoutRunUp": true}` in the ruleset's distance: it jumps that far in place of what `combat.jumping` (1.499) reads off its abilities, as far with a run-up, and without one as the ruleset's `standing` says, unless `withoutRunUp` keeps it the whole distance. What a condition's `jumps` multiplies or adds still does. 5e's bulette, Standing Leap: 30 feet long and 15 high, with or without a running start.

Worn by a mount (Capability API 1.717).
